<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算自测试使经典验证者能对单个计算受限证明者的量子寄存器施加控制。本文旨在实现资源高效的BQP委托：现有方案资源开销较大，作者希望达到电路模型中的准线性总资源，将多方设置中的相关成果迁移到单证明者设置。

Method: 基于LWE假设构建新的计算自测试，用于证明者量子态的认证；该自测试支持对单量子比特Clifford可观测量σ_X、σ_Y、σ_Z、(σ_Y-σ_X)/√2、(σ_Y+σ_X)/√2的张量积态进行可验证随机远程态制备，具常数鲁棒性。利用该自测试对Broadbent（ToC 2018）的高效验证协议进行去量子化。进一步将Kalai等（STOC 2023）提出的编译器（将任意非局域博弈转为单证明者论证系统）应用于Coladangelo等（ToC 2024）自测试的修改版本，从而在单证明者设置中复现其结果。

Result: 得到首个电路模型中总资源准线性的BQP论证系统，基于LWE假设，委托g门电路总资源为O(poly(λ, log g)·g)，λ为LWE安全参数；新自测试实现常数鲁棒的可验证随机远程态制备，验证误差与制备量子比特数无关；在单证明者设置中成功复现多方设置下的既有结果。

Conclusion: 通过新计算自测试与编译器技术，在单证明者设置下以LWE假设为基础实现了资源高效的BQP委托论证系统，达到准线性总资源并具常数鲁棒性，将多方自测试成果推广至单证明者框架。

Abstract: Computational self-testing gives a classical verifier command over the quantum register of a single computationally bounded prover. We use this framework to construct the first argument system for BQP with quasilinear total resource requirements in the circuit model. Our argument system is based on the learning with errors (LWE) assumption and requires total resources of $O(\mathrm{poly}(λ, \log g)\cdot g)$ for delegating a circuit with $g$ gates, where $λ$ is the LWE security parameter. This is achieved by constructing a new computational self-test for certifying the prover's quantum state and using it to dequantize the efficient verification protocol of Broadbent (ToC 2018). Specifically, this self-test enables the verifiable, random remote state preparation of tensor product states of the single-qubit Clifford observables $σ_X, σ_Y, σ_Z, (σ_Y-σ_X)/\sqrt{2}$ and $(σ_Y+σ_X)/\sqrt{2}$, with constant robustness: the verification error is independent of the number of prepared qubits. This approach was first proposed by Coladangelo et al. (ToC 2024) in the multi-prover setting. We replicate their result in the single-prover setting by applying the compiler proposed by Kalai et al. (STOC 2023)---which turns any nonlocal game into a single-prover argument system---to a modified version of their self-test.

</details>


### [95] [Optimal Quantum-Classical Separations for Exact Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38073)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Amin Shiraz Gilani,Nikhil S. Mande*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文否定了精确学习模型中关于随机与量子查询复杂度关系的长期猜想，构造出概念类使随机（或确定）查询复杂度达到量子查询复杂度的三次方级别，超越了Grover与Bernstein-Vazirani两类经典量子加速范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究成员查询下概念类的精确学习，核心动机是厘清确定性、随机与量子查询复杂度D(C)、R(C)、Q(C)之间的最优关系。该模型中的两类典型量子加速（Grover搜索与Bernstein-Vazirani）导致长期猜想R(C)=O(Q(C)^2+Q(C)logN)，作者旨在检验该猜想是否成立并刻画经典与量子查询复杂度的最优关系。

Method: 通过构造特定的概念类C与C'，分析其在成员查询模型下的确定性、随机与量子查询复杂度，从而给出与已知上界相匹配的下界。具体地，构造出满足R(C)=Ω(Q(C)^3 logN / logQ(C))的概念类C，以及满足D(C')=Ω(Q(C')^3 logN)的概念类C'，与Arunachalam等[Quantum'21]和Servedio-Gortler[SICOMP'04]的上界进行对比。

Result: 成功否定了原有猜想。构造的C满足R(C)=Ω(Q(C)^3 logN / logQ(C))，与Arunachalam等的随机上界在常数因子内匹配；构造的C'满足D(C')=Ω(Q(C')^3 logN)，与Servedio-Gortler的确定性上界匹配。这表明Arunachalam等的随机上界中的节省本质上依赖于随机性。

Conclusion: 本文刻画了精确学习中经典与量子查询复杂度的最优关系，证明随机/确定性与量子复杂度之间可存在三次方级别的分离。此外，这是首次表明学习中的量子加速可以超越Grover与Bernstein-Vazirani范式，揭示了更广泛的量子学习加速可能性。

Abstract: We study exact learning with membership queries for concept classes $\mathcal C\subseteq\{0,1\}^N$, focusing on the relationships among their deterministic, randomized, and quantum query complexities, denoted $\mathsf{D}(\mathcal C)$, $\mathsf{R}(\mathcal C)$, and $\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)$, respectively. The two canonical quantum speedups in this model are witnessed by Grover search and Bernstein-Vazirani, leading to the longstanding conjecture $$ \mathsf{R}(\mathcal C)=O(\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)^2+\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)\log N). $$ We first refute this conjecture by constructing concept classes $\mathcal C$ and $\mathcal C'$ satisfying \[ \mathsf{R}(\mathcal C)=Ω\!\left(\frac{\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)^3\log N}{\log \mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)}\right) \qquad\text{and}\qquad \mathsf{D}(\mathcal C')=Ω(\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C')^3\log N). \] The first bound matches the upper bound of Arunachalam et al.~[Quantum'21] up to constant factors, while the second matches the upper bound of Servedio and Gortler~[SICOMP'04]. In particular, this shows that the saving in the randomized upper bound of Arunachalam et al. fundamentally relies on randomness. Apart from characterizing the optimal relationship between classical and quantum query complexity, our results are the first to show that quantum speedups for learning can go beyond the Grover and Bernstein-Vazirani paradigms.

</details>


### [96] [Ramanujan quantum expanders from the Weil representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38075)
*Siddhartha Jain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对每个奇素数幂 q 与 D=q+1，构造无限族度为 D 的 Ramanujan 量子扩展图，通过 Weil 表示的奇不可约子表示转移 Morgenstern 的 Ramanujan Cayley 图；用 O(log²N) 基本门与 O(log N) 辅助 qudit 实现，并精确满足 2√(D−1)/D 奇异值界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Iyer、Jain、Jordan、Somma，FOCS 2026）的量子扩展图实现无法精确达到 Ramanujan 奇异值界，只能带加性误差。本文希望消除该加性误差，在确切电路实现下严格满足 2√(D−1)/D 界，同时保持高效的量子门与辅助量子比特开销。

Method: 以 Morgenstern 在 PSL₂(F_p) 上的 Ramanujan Cayley 图（p 为 q 的偶次幂）为源，通过 SL₂(F_p) 的 Weil 表示的奇不可约子表示进行转移/提升，构造度为 D=q+1 的无限族 Ramanujan 量子扩展图。实现上使用与 q 相关的固定有限门集，耗费 O(log²N) 基本门与 O(log N) 个辅助 qudit（N 为扩展图维度）。

Result: 对任意奇素数幂 q 与 D=q+1，得到无限族度为 D 的 Ramanujan 量子扩展图；维度为 N 时实现代价为 O(log²N) 基本门和 O(log N) 个辅助 qudit；在电路精确实现假设下，严格达到 2√(D−1)/D 的奇异值上界，无任何加性误差。

Conclusion: 该构造在量子扩展图规模与门复杂度保持高效的同时，首次（相对 FOCS 2026 工作）实现无加性误差的精确 Ramanujan 奇异值界，为严格 Ramanujan 量子扩展图提供了显式构造与高效量子线路实现。

Abstract: For every odd prime power $q$ and $D=q+1$, we construct an infinite family of Ramanujan quantum expanders of degree $D$. The construction transfers Morgenstern's Ramanujan Cayley graphs on $\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{F}_{p})$ for $p$ which is an even power of $q$, through the odd irreducible subrepresentation of the Weil representation of $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_{p})$. For a quantum expander of dimension $N$, our implementation uses $O(\log^2 N)$ elementary gates, and $O(\log N)$ ancilla qudits, using a fixed finite gate set depending on $q$. An advantage compared to the previous work of Iyer, Jain, Jordan, and Somma (FOCS 2026) is that assuming the quantum circuit is implemented exactly, we satisfy the $2\sqrt{D-1}/D$ singular value bound exactly without any additive error.

</details>


### [97] [A commuting operator self-test for exact entanglement embezzlement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38083)
*Connor Paddock,Simon Schmidt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了基于纠缠挪用（embezzlement）的广义非局域博弈变体中，最优commuting operator关联构成一个commuting operator自测试，给出commuting operator框架下首个精确纠缠挪用协议的关联自测试，且该关联无法用量子张量积模型实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索commuting operator框架下的自测试能力，特别是针对纠缠挪用协议，并揭示commuting operator模型与量子张量积模型在可实现关联上的本质区别。

Method: 基于Coladangelo双人广义非局域博弈的变体，证明其最优commuting operator关联是commuting operator自测试；核心技术依赖Harris关于精确纠缠挪用所涉及的Cuntz代数的唯一抽象态刻画。

Result: 证明了最优关联是commuting operator自测试，由于该关联要求精确纠缠挪用，其无法被量子张量积模型实现（即使局域空间允许无限维）。

Conclusion: 该结果为commuting operator自测试提供了一个实例，其中双方共享的Hilbert空间无法与局域测量算子兼容地分解为局域空间的张量积，凸显了commuting operator框架相对量子张量积模型的独特优势。

Abstract: We show that the optimal commuting operator correlation for a variant of Coladangelo's two-player generalized nonlocal game, based on the embezzlement of entanglement, is a commuting operator self-test. Our result gives the first correlation self-test for an exact entanglement embezzlement protocol in the commuting operator framework. Because the unique optimal correlation necessitates the exact embezzlement of entanglement, the correlation is unrealizable using quantum tensor-product models, even if the local spaces are allowed to be infinite dimensional. As such, our result provides an example of a commuting operator self-test where the Hilbert space shared by the two parties cannot be factored into a tensor-product of local spaces that is compatible with the local measurement operators. Our main proof technique relies on Harris' unique abstract state characterization of exact embezzlement of entanglement involving Cuntz algebras.

</details>


### [98] [Learning quantum symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38085)
*Isaac Holt,Sathyawageeswar Subramanian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将对称性学习从经典对象推广到量子对象，围绕StateHSP（态隐藏子群问题）在非阿贝尔情形取得突破：对正规子群及一大类非阿贝尔群给出高效量子算法，并提出更符合物理的Anyonic对称学习问题，通过投影表示线性化与线性纠错码的新联系归约到StateHSP，进而改进了混合qudit稳定子群学习算法，同时将框架扩展到幺正、哈密顿量及有限态集等量子对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典对称性发现已有Shor算法与隐藏子群问题（HSP）等强大量子工具，但量子对象自身的对称性学习尚缺乏系统理论。已有StateHSP仅覆盖阿贝尔群，且其"Bose"对称基于线性表示，而物理上纯态只定义到全局相位，二者存在不匹配，因此需要将理论推广到非阿贝尔情形并引入更物理自然的对称概念。

Method: 1）研究StateHSP，在隐藏子群为正规子群时，以及环境群属于一类广泛的非阿贝尔群时，构造高效量子算法，将原有一般理论从阿贝尔情形扩展出去；2）提出Anyonic态对称学习问题，用更物理的对称定义替代StateHSP的Bose对称；3）通过建立投影表示线性化与线性纠错码之间的新联系，将Anyonic问题归约到StateHSP求解；4）把幺正、哈密顿量与有限态集等对象的对称学习问题也归约到StateHSP实例。

Result: 获得了非阿贝尔StateHSP的正向结果：当隐藏子群正规、且环境群属于一大类非阿贝尔群时存在高效量子算法；给出了Anyonic态对称学习的有效量子算法，主要依赖投影表示线性化与线性纠错码的新联系；作为应用，得到适用于任意局部维度混合qudit态的改进稳定子群学习算法；同时为幺正、哈密顿量和有限态集等量子对象给出了高效对称学习算法。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子对称性学习理论从阿贝尔推广到非阿贝尔领域，并通过Anyonic问题与纠错码的联系，把物理上更自然的对称概念纳入可计算框架；其归约思路具通用性，可统一处理多种量子对象的对称学习，并改进了混合qudit稳定子群学习，表明StateHSP可作为量子对称性学习的核心归约枢纽。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms are powerful tools for finding symmetries of classical objects, most famously through Shor's algorithm and the framework of the Hidden Subgroup Problem (HSP). In this work, we study quantum algorithms for learning symmetries of quantum objects. Our starting point is the recently introduced State Hidden Subgroup Problem (StateHSP), a quantum generalisation of HSP in which the task is to learn the symmetry subgroup of a quantum state. We obtain positive results for non-abelian StateHSP, giving efficient quantum algorithms whenever the hidden subgroup is normal and for ambient groups belonging to a broad class of non-abelian groups, extending the previous general theory beyond the abelian setting.
  StateHSP learns \emph{Bose} symmetries, associated with linear representations, whereas physically equivalent pure states are defined only up to global phase. Motivated by this, we introduce a new \emph{Anyonic} state symmetry learning problem based on a more physically natural notion of symmetry than that considered by StateHSP. We give an efficient quantum algorithm by reducing the problem to StateHSP, where the reduction relies on a new connection between linearisations of projective representations and linear error-correcting codes. As an application, we obtain an improved algorithm for learning stabiliser groups that applies to mixed qudit states of arbitrary local dimension. Finally, we introduce symmetry learning problems for other quantum objects, including unitaries, Hamiltonians, and finite collections of states, and obtain efficient quantum algorithms by reducing them to instances of StateHSP.

</details>


### [99] [Optimal Ground-State Preparation with a Guiding State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38091)
*Stacey Jeffery,Rolando D. Somma,Freek Witteveen,Ronald de Wolf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出基态制备的严格算法：在已知基态能量估计、谱隙与引导态重叠度条件下，用 $O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/\gamma\delta)$ 次 $U=e^{iH}$ 与 $A$ 及其逆，获得基态 $|\psi_0\rangle$ 的 $\varepsilon$-近似，并证明该界最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态能量估计已有近期工作 [JW26, SdW26]，但缺少与之配套的基态量子态制备算法；本文旨在补全这一环节，并达到对数精度与引导态重叠度、谱隙倒数成反比的最优复杂度。

Method: 设定：唯一基态能量 $E_0$、估计误差 $|\tilde E_0-E_0|\le\delta$、谱隙 $\ge3\delta$、引导态重叠 $\ge\gamma$。给出两种算法：(1) 交织幅度放大与误差缩减（[HMdW03] 风格）；(2) 基于 transducer 的组合。

Result: 两种算法均使用 $O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/\gamma\delta)$ 次 $U=e^{iH}$ 与 $A$ 及其逆，输出基态 $|\psi_0\rangle$ 的 $\varepsilon$-近似。

Conclusion: 结合前期能量估计结果，本文给出基态制备的最优上界 $O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/\gamma\delta)$，与能量估计部分共同构成完整的基态问题复杂度刻画。

Abstract: Suppose a Hamiltonian $H$ has a unique ground state $|ψ_0\rangle$ with an eigenvalue $E_0$, and we have an estimate $\tilde{E}_0$ such that $|\tilde{E}_0-E_0|\leqδ$, and there is a gap of at least $3δ$ between $E_0$ and all other eigenvalues. Suppose we have a unitary $A$ available that can produce a "guiding state'" $A|0\rangle$ that has overlap at least $γ$ with $|ψ_0\rangle$. We show how to obtain an $\varepsilon$-approximation of $|ψ_0\rangle$ using $O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/γδ)$ applications of $U=e^{iH}$ and $A$, and their inverses. We give two different algorithms, one based on interleaving amplitude amplification and error-reduction in the style of [HMdW03], and one using the composition of transducers. This paper is the state-preparation follow-up to our two recent ground-state-energy estimation papers [JW26, SdW26]. Combined, our results show an optimal $O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/γδ)$ upper bound for ground state preparation.

</details>


### [100] [Resource Compatibility in Optimal Quantum Symmetry Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38134)
*Yimeng Cao,Ranyiliu Chen,Chengkai Zhu,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究对称性检验是否能不使用其天然关联的量子资源而达到最优，通过表示论给出平行子群-对-Haar检验中零第一类错误协议的定量逆定理，将超额第二类错误下界表示为两个非负惩罚之和，并据此刻画最优协议的支撑与权重锁定现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性检验通常需要消耗与对称性天然关联的量子资源（如相干性），但一个根本性问题尚未明确：能否在不使用该资源的情况下仍达到最优检验性能？作者希望建立一般性的表示论框架，量化"无资源"所付出的性能代价，并判断哪些情形下无资源最优是可能的。

Method: 针对平行子群-对-Haar检验，作者对所有具有零第一类错误的协议建立定量表示论逆定理：超额第二类错误下界为两项惩罚之和——一项来自次优子群类型上的权重，另一项来自各类型内Schur profile相对其类型极值profile的偏离。在最优点两项惩罚须同时为零，从而对每个最优系统边缘态导出支撑锁定与权重锁定。随后将该框架应用于具体群：维度d≥2的对角环面、正交子群以及三能级Clifford群。

Result: 最优时两项惩罚消失，得到最优系统边缘态的支撑与权重锁定；锁定集与自由边缘态集不相交排除了无资源最优性，但仅重叠不足以证明可达性。对维度d≥2的对角环面，无相干最优检验仅当查询次数为一次时可行；作者给出所有非相干系统边缘态协议下精确的最优第二类错误，以及达到无限制最优所需的最小系统边缘态相干量。对维度d≥2的正交子群，实协议在任意查询次数下均达到最优。对三能级Clifford群，Wigner正最优协议在三次与四次查询时存在，但五至九次查询不存在；九次以上是否存在仍未解决。

Conclusion: 最优对称性检验的资源需求（子群依赖且查询次数依赖）与最优系统边缘态的结构约束紧密相关。该表示论逆定理为判断无资源最优性提供了必要条件与量化工具，并通过若干具体群例揭示了资源需求随查询次数和群结构变化的非平凡模式。

Abstract: Can a symmetry be tested optimally without using the quantum resource naturally associated with it? For parallel subgroup-versus-Haar testing, we derive a quantitative representation-theoretic converse for every protocol with zero type-I error: the excess type-II error is bounded below by the sum of two nonnegative penalties, one for weight on suboptimal subgroup types and one for deviations of within-type Schur profiles from their typewise extremal profiles. At optimality, both penalties vanish, yielding support and weight locking for every optimal system marginal. Disjointness of the resulting locked set and the free marginal set rules out resource-free optimality, whereas overlap alone does not establish attainability. For the diagonal torus in dimension $d\geq 2$, optimal testing without coherence is possible only with a single query. We determine both the exact optimal type-II error over all protocols with incoherent system marginals and the minimum system-marginal coherence required to attain the unrestricted optimum. For the orthogonal subgroup in dimension $d\geq 2$, a real protocol attains the optimum at every query number. For the qutrit Clifford group, Wigner-positive optimal protocols exist at three and four queries but not from five through nine; whether they exist beyond nine queries remains open. Together, these results relate the subgroup- and query-dependent resource requirements of optimal symmetry testing to structural constraints on optimal system marginals.

</details>


### [101] [The exact strong converse exponent for private communication over quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38144)
*Hao-Chung Cheng,Christoph Hirche,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了有限维量子窃听信道下密钥传输与生成的精确强逆指数，引入其正则化刻画该指数的Rényi私有信息，并给出关于普通私有信息的积分表示与连续性界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画量子窃听信道中秘密密钥传输与生成的可靠性与保密性在高码率区的衰减行为，即确定私有容量以上的强逆指数。

Method: 以对理想密钥的平方保真度联合度量可靠性与保密性；引入一种新的Rényi私有信息，用其正则化刻画强逆指数；给出该量关于普通私有信息的精确积分表示，并导出均匀连续性界限。

Result: 获得了一般有限维量子窃听信道的精确强逆指数；正则化Rényi私有信息在Rényi阶趋于1时收敛到私有容量；证明对任意窃听信道，在私有容量以上任意码率下保真度呈指数衰减。

Conclusion: Rényi私有信息的正则化可完整刻画量子窃听信道的强逆行为，统一解释了私有容量以上码率的指数级保密性衰减。

Abstract: We determine the exact strong converse exponent for secret-key transmission and generation over general finite-dimensional quantum wiretap channels, measuring reliability and secrecy jointly by squared fidelity to an ideal secret key. We introduce a novel Renyi private information whose regularization characterizes this exponent. An exact integral representation in terms of ordinary private information gives a uniform continuity bound, showing that the regularized quantity converges to private capacity as the Renyi order approaches one. This establishes for any wiretap channel an exponential fidelity decay at every rate above private capacity.

</details>


### [102] [Query-optimal unitary channel tomography in diamond distance with parallel access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38145)
*Entong He,Zihao Li,Yuxiang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种并行量子查询协议，用 $\mathcal{O}(d^2/\varepsilon)$ 次并行查询即可在菱形距离内学习 $d$ 维未知酉信道，达到下界，从而关闭了酉信道层析中并行与顺序策略之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子过程层析中长期存在一个开放问题：能否在严格并行查询下，以与顺序查询相同的效率学习酉信道。直觉上顺序查询可利用自适应性，而并行查询无法调整策略；从高阶量子操作视角看，并行策略可被顺序策略模拟，反之不成立，因此顺序策略似乎更强。本文旨在挑战这一直觉并解决该开放问题。

Method: 作者设计了一种新的并行学习协议，用于学习未知 $d$ 维酉信道，目标是在菱形距离内达到精度 $\varepsilon$，查询复杂度为 $\mathcal{O}(d^2/\varepsilon)$ 次并行查询。

Result: 该协议的并行查询复杂度 $\mathcal{O}(d^2/\varepsilon)$ 与 [Haah, Kothari, O'Donnell, and Tang, FOCS '23] 给出的下界相匹配。作为应用，该协议在边界区域的量子信道层析中达到最优查询复杂度，并提升了费米子线性光学层析中已知最佳协议的查询效率。

Conclusion: 并行查询策略在酉信道层析中可以达到与顺序查询相同的效率，关闭了二者之间的差距；该结果还对量子信道层析和费米子线性光学层析等应用带来改进。

Abstract: In the study of quantum process tomography, it has remained open how to learn a unitary channel in diamond distance with strictly parallel queries as efficiently as with sequential queries. Sequential queries allow one to exploit adaptivity to refine the estimate and adjust the learning strategy accordingly, whereas such adjustments are impossible for parallel queries. From the perspective of higher-order quantum operations, any parallel learning strategy can be simulated by a sequential one, whereas the converse does not hold in general, suggesting that sequential learning strategies are potentially more powerful. Contrary to this intuition, we present a new protocol for learning an unknown $d$-dimensional unitary channel to within $\varepsilon$ in diamond distance using $\mathcal{O}(d^2/\varepsilon)$ parallel queries, matching the lower bound presented in [Haah, Kothari, O'Donnell, and Tang, FOCS '23]. Our protocol thus closes the gap between parallel and sequential strategies for unitary channel tomography. As applications, it achieves optimal query complexity for boundary-regime quantum channel tomography and improves the query efficiency of the best-known protocol for tomography of fermionic linear optics.

</details>


### [103] [Optimal Tolerant Testing of Lindbladian Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38160)
*Jinge Bao,Yiyi Cai,Zhong-Xia Shang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究含噪声量子系统中耗散强度的容错判定问题：在仅能进行无记忆前向演化的条件下，对未知、时不变的Lindblad生成元（跳跃算符局部但允许任意重叠），以归一化Frobenius范数度量，区分耗散强度≤ε₁与≥ε₂，给出最优演化时间算法与匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化耗散对控制量子噪声与刻画开放系统动力学至关重要。实际实验中残余噪声难以完全抑制，因此需要判定耗散强度是否处于规定的容差阈值内。已有耗散检验工作通常要求跳跃算符的度有界，难以覆盖更广泛的实验场景，故需扩展到容错设定并去除有界度限制。

Method: 针对未知、时不变、强度有界的Lindblad生成元，跳跃算符为局部但重叠不受限；仅使用无记忆前向演化访问。构造算法在总演化时间O(ε₂/(ε₂-ε₁)²)内，以常数成功概率区分耗散强度≤ε₁与≥ε₂（0≤ε₁<ε₂），强度用经典耗散子的归一化Frobenius范数度量；并证明匹配下界Ω(ε₂/(ε₂-ε₁)²)。

Result: 算法对所有非平凡阈值均可在O(ε₂/(ε₂-ε₁)²)总演化时间内以常数成功概率完成区分任务；下界Ω(ε₂/(ε₂-ε₁)²)表明该复杂度即使对自适应协议也是最优的。结果同时去除了有界度要求。

Conclusion: 本文把耗散检验推广到容错（tolerant）设定，并消除有界度约束，使该框架适用于更广泛的实验相关场景，且复杂度达到最优。

Abstract: Quantifying dissipation is essential for controlling quantum noise and characterizing open-system dynamics. In practical experimental settings, residual noise may still persist despite efforts to suppress it, making it important to determine whether the dissipative strength remains within a prescribed tolerance threshold. We study this question for an unknown, time-independent Lindblad generator with bounded strength, where the jump operators are local but with unrestricted overlap, using only memoryless forward-evolution access. The task is to distinguish dissipative strength of at most $\varepsilon_1$ from strength of at least $\varepsilon_2$, for $0 \leq \varepsilon_1 < \varepsilon_2$, measured in the canonical dissipator's normalized Frobenius norm. We give an algorithm that solves this task in total evolution time $O(\varepsilon_2/(\varepsilon_2-\varepsilon_1)^2)$ for all nontrivial thresholds, with constant success probability, and provide a matching lower bound $Ω(\varepsilon_2/(\varepsilon_2-\varepsilon_1)^2)$ that establishes optimality even for adaptive protocols. This extends dissipation testing to the tolerant setting and eliminates the bounded-degree requirement, making the framework applicable to a broader range of experimentally relevant settings.

</details>


### [104] [When Classical Correlations Certify Entanglement Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38168)
*Takeru Utsumi,Yota Tachibana,Yoshifumi Nakata,Takaya Matsuura,Ryuji Takagi,Francesco Buscemi,Masato Koashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明纠缠与可区分性的联系可推广到一般测量：只要POVM满足"不可约性"（比互补性更宽松的条件），就能建立单侧局域转化到最大纠缠态的不保真度与态分辨失败概率之间的定量关系，并刻画两者同时消失的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠与态可区分性分别是量子基础中的核心议题，二者通过互补基下的测量相关联，并支撑量子纠错等信息处理任务。但这一联系是否可脱离互补性假设、推广到一般测量，此前基本悬而未决。作者希望找到更普适的条件，使纠缠—可区分性联系成立，并借此统一纠缠蒸馏、量子纠错与测量权衡等方向。

Method: 引入"测量诱导的不可约性"作为核心条件——它比互补性宽松得多。对满足该不可约性条件的POVM，建立单侧局域转化为最大纠缠态的不保真度与态分辨失败概率之间的定量不等式关系；进而分析两误差可同时趋于零的充要条件；并由此推导测量理论中"信息增益—不可逆性"与"可观测噪声—扰动"两类历史路径之间的新型权衡关系。

Result: 证实了纠缠与可区分性的联系确实可推广到一般测量，无需互补性假设；给出不保真度与分辨失败概率的定量关系及二者同时消失的刻画；导出连接两种测量权衡范式的新权衡关系；并在纠缠蒸馏与量子纠错中获得直接应用，其误差可通过实验可及的局域测量估计经典输入—输出关联来认证。

Conclusion: "不可约性"为纠缠与可区分性之间提供了超越互补性的统一桥梁，构成纠缠蒸馏、量子纠错与测量权衡的共同框架，同时拓展了量子测量理论，衔接了信息增益—不可逆性与噪声—扰动两条历史脉络。

Abstract: Entanglement and state distinguishability have long been central topics in quantum foundations. While each has developed into a rich subject in its own right, they can be connected through measurements in complementary bases. This connection provides insights into quantum and classical correlations and underlies many information-processing tasks, most notably quantum error correction (QEC). However, it has remained largely open whether the connection between entanglement and distinguishability extends to general measurements without assuming complementarity. We answer this question in the affirmative: the key is irreducibility induced by measurements, a much more relaxed condition than complementarity. Specifically, for POVMs satisfying an irreducibility condition, we establish a quantitative relation between the infidelity of one-sided local transformation into a maximally entangled state and the failure probability in state discrimination. We then characterize when both errors can vanish simultaneously. Our results have direct applications to entanglement distillation and QEC. Their errors can be certified by estimating classical input-output correlations from experimentally accessible local measurements, without requiring complementarity. The results also extend to quantum measurement theory. We derive novel trade-off relations that bridge two historically distinct approaches: information gain--irreversibility and observable noise--disturbance. Our connection between entanglement and distinguishability via irreducibility thus offers a common framework for entanglement distillation, QEC, and measurement trade-offs.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Topological Band Theory for High-Dimensional Parameter Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35977)
*Vivek Chakrabhavi,Walt Chavarria,Jonathan J. Heckman,Steven Rayan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究外加规范势的绝热流参数构成高维流形时的量子霍尔体系拓扑能带理论，揭示拓扑不变量由第一陈类及更高阶曲率不变量构成，并给出基于亏格2 Bolza曲面的具体实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子霍尔体系的拓扑能带理论主要建立在二维参数空间之上，拓扑不变量较为简单。然而对于亏格g>1的黎曼曲面上的强关联物质，以及维度为4ℓ+2=2p的紧致定向流形耦合p-形式规范势的情形，参数空间具有高维结构，其拓扑不变量远比低维情形丰富，需要拓展现有理论框架。

Method: 作者从拓扑能带理论出发，考虑外加规范势的绝热流参数构成高维流形的量子霍尔体系，系统分析定义于参数空间上的拓扑不变量。重点研究第一陈类（曲率）与更高阶曲率不变量，并推导这些高阶曲率不变量对应于Kubo型公式的拓扑保护贡献，该贡献由物理流算子的关联函数构造。

Result: 研究表明，高维参数空间下的拓扑不变量显著丰富于二维情形，包含第一陈类以及更高阶曲率不变量；这些高阶曲率不变量对应Kubo型公式中的拓扑保护项，可由物理流算子的关联函数构建。基于亏格2 Bolza黎曼曲面的双曲能带理论给出了显式实例，且该情形近期已在合成维度平台上被模拟。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子霍尔体系的拓扑能带理论推广至具有高维绝热流参数流形的情形，统一处理了高阶亏格黎曼曲面上的强关联物质与高维定向流形耦合微分形式规范势的问题，并建立了高阶曲率不变量与Kubo型公式的联系，为合成维度平台实验提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study the topological band theory of quantum Hall systems in which the adiabatic flux parameters of an external gauge potential form a high-dimensional manifold. This situation arises both for strongly correlated matter on spatial Riemann surfaces of genus $g > 1$, as well as in the formal study of matter on compact oriented manifolds of dimension $4\ell+2 = 2p$ coupled to $p$-form gauge potentials. In these cases, the underlying topological invariants defined over the parameter space are significantly richer than the low-dimensional (i.e., two-dimensional) case and involve both the curvature (i.e., first Chern class) as well as higher order curvature invariants. These higher curvature invariants correspond to topologically protected contributions to Kubo-like formulae, constructed from correlation functions of the physical current operators. We illustrate these general considerations with an explicit example from hyperbolic band theory based on the genus-two Bolza Riemann surface, a case which has recently been simulated on a synthetic-dimension platform.

</details>


### [106] [Magnetoplasmon molecule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36036)
*A. V. Larionov,A. V. Gorbunov,A. B. Van'kov,D. A. Shchigarev,L. V. Kulik,V. Umansky*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究外磁场下二维电子系统中的一种本征四粒子激发——"磁等离激元分子"，它由两个自旋相反的电子与两个费米空穴构成，并考察其与磁激子凝聚体的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索外磁场中二维电子系统内除已知集体激发之外的新型本征多粒子激发，并理解其与磁激子凝聚体等强关联态的耦合行为。

Method: 在二维电子系统加外磁场的理论框架下，构造并分析四粒子激发态的结构（两个电子、两个自旋相反的费米空穴），将其作为"磁等离激元分子"；进而分析该分子与磁激子凝聚体之间的相互作用。

Result: 观测（识别）到该本征四粒子激发；其中两个电子和一个费米空穴参与电子系统的集体等离子体振荡，第二个费米空穴则中和多余电子电荷。

Conclusion: 磁等离激元分子是二维磁化电子系统中的一种新型本征激发，其与磁激子凝聚体的相互作用可能构成研究多粒子复合激发与凝聚态耦合的重要途径。

Abstract: We investigate a two-dimensional electron system in an external magnetic field. An intrinsic four-particle excitation of the electron system, which we term a magnetoplasmon molecule, is observed. The molecule comprises two identical electrons and two Fermi holes with opposite spins. Both electrons and one Fermi hole participate in the collective plasma oscillations of the electron system, whereas the second Fermi hole neutralizes the excess electronic charge. We examine the interaction between the magnetoplasmon molecule and a magnetoexciton condensate.

</details>


### [107] [Topology Obstructs Nodeless Excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36248)
*Lumen Eek*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文揭示能带拓扑可阻碍最低能激子具有无节点1s型包络波函数：激子包络波函数是价-导跃迁丛的截面，其拓扑可强制动量空间零点，并据此分类了不同维度、实/复跃迁丛下拓扑强制的激子节点结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预期最低能激子具有无节点的1s型包络波函数，但作者质疑能带拓扑是否会对此构成阻碍，即拓扑是否会对激子波函数和谱施加直接约束。

Method: 将激子包络波函数视为价-导跃迁丛的截面，利用其特征类分析动量空间零点的奇偶性或带符号总指标；以一个PT对称三带最小模型（秩二跃迁丛、Euler数ν^e=2）为例，直接求解含接触相互作用与Rytova-Keldysh相互作用的Bethe-Salpeter方程；并分类d≤3维实与复跃迁丛中拓扑强制的激子节点结构。

Result: 最低激子包含两个零点，每个指标为+1，且其束缚强度弱于拓扑平庸对应物；同时给出了适用于d≤3维实与复跃迁丛的拓扑强制激子节点结构分类。

Conclusion: 跃迁丛的拓扑构成对激子波函数和光谱的直接约束，表明能带拓扑可从根本上改变最低能激子的节点结构与束缚性质。

Abstract: Lowest-energy excitons are usually expected to have nodeless $1s$-like envelope wavefunctions. We show that band topology can obstruct this expectation. The exciton envelope wavefunction is a section of the valence-conduction transition bundle, whose topology can enforce momentum-space zeros, with their parity or signed total index fixed by the corresponding characteristic class. We exemplify this behavior in a minimal $PT$-symmetric three-band model with a rank-two transition bundle characterized by Euler number $ν^e=2$. By directly solving the Bethe-Salpeter equation with both contact and Rytova-Keldysh interactions, we find that the lowest exciton contains two zeros, each with index $+1$, and is less strongly bound than its topologically trivial counterpart. We further classify topology-enforced exciton nodal structures for real and complex transition bundles in dimensions $d \leq 3$. Our results establish the topology of the transition bundle as a direct constraint on exciton wavefunctions and spectra.

</details>


### [108] [Unveiling Three-Dimensional Skyrmion Transitions Through Vortices and Monopoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36261)
*Melissa E. Henderson,Daichi Kurebayashi,Benjamin Heacock,Wangchun Chen,Charles W. Clark,David G. Cory,Dusan Sarenac,Shannon Watson,Jonathan S. White,Lisa DeBeer-Schmitt,Oleg A. Tretiakov,Dmitry A. Pushin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文首次利用中子散射层析技术，在Co₈Zn₈Mn₄块体样品平衡相中实验可视化了三维拓扑Q=0的磁斯格明子结构，观察到亚稳斯格明子及复合拓扑客体，并揭示涡旋-反涡旋晶格通过半子与磁单极耦合介导的拓扑转变路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁斯格明子通常被视为具有整数拓扑电荷的二维客体，但向三维弦的拓展可实现超越平面框架的复合结构与器件功能。然而，由于缺乏块体探测手段，这类高维拓扑结构及其实现一直未被实验证实，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用中子散射层析技术（neutron scattering tomography），在Co₈Zn₈Mn₄样品的平衡相中对三维拓扑Q=0的斯格明子结构进行实验可视化，并分析无序斯格明子态中的亚稳态结构及拓扑转变过程。

Result: 首次实验观测到三维Q=0斯格明子结构；无序态中出现了块体体系中新颖的亚稳斯格明子（skyrmioniums）及复合拓扑客体；涡旋-反涡旋晶格介导拓扑变化，并通过半子（merons）与磁单极（monopoles）的耦合实现前所未有的转变路径。

Conclusion: 块体Q=0准粒子与半子介导动力学的实现，为高维斯宾电子学框架开辟了道路，可推动多位编码架构、单向输运方案及磁单极介导调控等应用。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions represent vortex-like spin configurations that provide a robust platform for next-generation spintronic technologies. Although they are often treated as two-dimensional objects with integer topological charge, their extension into three-dimensional strings realizes other composite structures with unique device functionalities that transcend planar frameworks. Unfortunately, a lack of bulk probes has failed to realize such higher-dimensional topological structures and their implementations. Here, we report the first experimental visualization of three-dimensional topological $Q = 0$ skyrmion structures using neutron scattering tomography techniques across the equilibrium phase of a Co$_8$Zn$_8$Mn$_4$ sample. Disordered skyrmion states reveal metastable skyrmioniums and composite topological objects novel to bulk systems. Vortex-antivortex lattices mediate changes in topology, with unprecedented transition pathways via a coupling of merons and monopoles. The present realization of bulk Q = 0 quasiparticles and meron-mediated dynamics paves the way for higher-dimensional spintronic frameworks through multi-bit encoding architectures, unidirectional transport schemes, and monopole-mediated controls.

</details>


### [109] [Effects of Longitudinal Spin Current Density Gradient on Spin-Orbit Torque Switching of Perpendicular Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36489)
*Guowen Gong,Qianbiao Liu,Lijun Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过微磁学模拟与实验证明，纵向自旋流密度梯度无法替代纵向磁场实现垂直磁各向异性器件的确定性自旋轨道力矩翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋轨道力矩（SOT）翻转垂直磁各向异性（PMA）正常金属/铁磁异质结时，通常需要纵向磁场来保证确定性翻转，而施加磁场不利于器件集成。因此人们希望用纵向自旋流梯度（如来源于厚度、组分或宽度梯度）替代纵向磁场。这一方案是否可行一直是一个关键悬而未决的问题。

Method: 采用微磁学模拟结合实验验证，考察任意真实纵向自旋流密度梯度下的SOT翻转行为；并将自旋流脉冲伴随的横向有效场（奥斯特场与类场力矩之和）纳入分析。

Result: 研究显示，真实的纵向自旋流密度梯度只能改变磁畴的成核以及脉冲时序敏感的振荡，从而形成皮秒量级的条状非确定性翻转窗口；即使计入横向有效场，该结论依然稳健。这种非确定性翻转在存在器件间差异、写电流脉冲漂移及器件参数热涨落时会导致应用失效。

Conclusion: 纵向自旋流密度梯度不是纵向磁场的有效替代方案；实验表明，垂直自旋在垂直自旋力矩器件确定性翻转中远比纵向自旋流梯度有效。

Abstract: It has remained a critical open question as to whether a longitudinal spin current gradient (e.g., due to a gradient in the thickness, composition, or width) can replace the longitudinal magnetic field required for deterministic spin-orbit torque switching of normal metal/ferromagnet heterostructures with perpendicular magnetic anisotropy. Here, we report robust micromagnetic and experimental evidence that any realistic longitudinal spin-current density gradient cannot be an effective replacement for a longitudinal magnetic field to enable deterministic switching of a perpendicular magnetic anisotropy device. Instead, the longitudinal spin current density can only modify the nucleation and pulse-timing-sensitive oscillations of magnetic domains and thus the picosecond-scale strip-like indeterministic switching windows. The same conclusions hold robustly when the transverse effective field associated with the spin-current pulse (the sum of the Oersted field and field-like torque) is taken into account. The indeterministic switching prevents applications in the presence of finite device-to-device variations and drifts in write current pulse and thermal fluctuations of device parameters. We also experimentally show that, instead of a longitudinal spin current density gradient, perpendicular spins are much more effective in deterministic switching of perpendicular spin torque devices.

</details>


### [110] [Magnetic-field-activated transport from band geometry in gapped nodal-line semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36606)
*L. Medel Onofre,L. E. Sosa-Arias,A. Martín-Ruiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作建立了带隙节线半金属磁输运的非微扰半经典理论，完整保留贝里曲率与轨道磁矩修正的磁场依赖，给出了强磁场下可解析的内禀与耗散电导，并预言磁场激活的几何输运、非线性磁响应与平面霍尔效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁输运理论多基于微扰磁场展开，难以描述节线半金属中贝里曲率与轨道磁矩在有限磁场下的强非线性重构效应；同时节线几何导致的方位角相消使得内禀霍尔电流在零场下缺失，其磁场激活机制尚缺乏统一、非微扰的描述框架。

Method: 从最小两带模型出发，在semiclassical regime下构建非微扰半经典输运理论，保留贝里曲率和轨道磁矩修正对磁场的完整依赖，推导内禀霍尔响应与耗散费米面电导的精确表达式，其对磁场适用于所有阶。

Result: 轨道磁矩重构了几何活跃费米面，破坏节线几何的方位角相消，从而激活零场下不存在内禀霍尔电流；霍尔响应随化学势呈显著非单调依赖，并表现出强烈非线性磁场演化。费米面电导在弱场极限回归Drude行为，随场增强获得二次乃至非微扰修正，并出现场诱导输运各向异性，在中间场产生可测量的平面霍尔效应；当费米能级靠近节线时，内禀与耗散响应均显著增强。

Conclusion: 磁场激活的几何输运是节线半金属的特征性签名；该工作提供了超越微扰磁场展开的统一磁输运描述框架，并给出轨道磁矩物理的实验可及信号。

Abstract: We develop a nonperturbative semiclassical theory of magnetotransport in gapped nodal-line semimetals, retaining the full magnetic-field dependence of Berry-curvature and orbital-magnetic-moment corrections. Starting from a minimal two-band model, we derive exact expressions for both the intrinsic Hall response and the dissipative Fermi-surface conductivity, valid to all orders in the magnetic field within the semiclassical regime. We show that the orbital magnetic moment reconstructs the geometrically active Fermi surface, breaking the azimuthal cancellation imposed by the nodal-line geometry and thereby activating an intrinsic Hall current that is absent at zero magnetic field. The Hall response exhibits a pronounced nonmonotonic dependence on the chemical potential and a strongly nonlinear magnetic-field evolution, reflecting the redistribution of geometrically active states around the nodal ring. We further demonstrate that the Fermi-surface conductivities recover the conventional Drude behavior in the weak-field limit, while acquiring quadratic and ultimately nonperturbative magnetic-field corrections together with a field-induced transport anisotropy that gives rise to a measurable planar Hall effect at intermediate fields. Both the intrinsic and dissipative responses are strongly enhanced when the Fermi level lies close to the nodal ring, where Berry curvature and orbital magnetic moment are largest. Our results identify magnetic-field-activated geometric transport as a characteristic signature of nodal-line semimetals and provide a unified framework for describing magnetotransport beyond perturbative magnetic-field expansions, together with experimentally accessible signatures of orbital-magnetic-moment physics.

</details>


### [111] [Linear and nonlinear transport responses of topological nodal-line semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36664)
*L. E. Sosa-Arias,A. Martín-Ruiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了有限静电势垒下拓扑节线半金属的电荷输运，发现受节线拓扑保护的正入射Klein隧穿行为，并推导了线性与非线性（含纵向和Hall）电导的一般表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑节线半金属具有动量空间闭合环状能带交叉，在满足\(\mathcal{PT}\)对称时无需自旋轨道耦合即可稳定，产生鼓面表面态和非常规输运响应。本文旨在探究有限静电势垒下该类材料的电荷输运特性，揭示其对势垒参数及\(\mathcal{PT}\)破缺质量项的依赖，以寻找节线拓扑的清晰输运特征。

Method: 求解节线半金属中势垒的散射问题（两端为同一平衡材料）；利用Landauer-Büttiker形式推导线性与非线性电导（含纵向与Hall分量）的一般表达式，并在零温和有限温度下进行解析与数值计算。

Result: 势垒透射呈现由节线拓扑保护的Klein隧穿行为（正入射情形），其根源为Berry曲率诱导的动量锁定。得到电导随势垒高度、宽度及\(\mathcal{PT}\)破缺质量项的变化规律；识别出非线性贡献显著增强、横向Hall流出现的不同输运区间。

Conclusion: 横向Hall流与非线性电导增强等现象为节线拓扑提供了明确的输运信号，并为相关器件应用指明潜在方向。

Abstract: Topological nodal-line semimetals are three-dimensional quantum materials characterized by band crossings that form closed loops in momentum space. In $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric realizations, these nodal rings are stabilized in the absence of spin-orbit coupling, giving rise to drumhead surface states and unconventional transport responses. In this work, we study charge transport across a nodal-line semimetal containing a finite electrostatic barrier, with both leads described by the same equilibrium material. By solving the corresponding scattering problem, we show that the transmission across the barrier exhibits {Klein-tunneling behavior protected at normal incidence by the nodal topology}, despite the extended nodal-line dispersion, which can be traced back to Berry-curvature-induced momentum locking. Using the Landauer-Büttiker formalism, we derive general expressions for the linear and nonlinear conductances, including both longitudinal and Hall components, and evaluate them at zero and finite temperature. Our analytical and numerical results elucidate the dependence of the conductance on barrier height and width, as well as on a $\mathcal{PT}$-breaking mass term. We identify distinct transport regimes in which nonlinear contributions are strongly enhanced and transverse Hall currents emerge, providing clear transport signatures of nodal-line topology and suggesting potential routes toward device applications.

</details>


### [112] [Interlayer coupling between twisted graphenes through atomically-precise barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36703)
*Yueyang Wang,Ben Fuller,Hongxia Xue,Tianyu Zhang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Vladimir Falko,Dong-Keun Ki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究提出利用双栅器件与厚度可调的超薄hBN间隔层，在扭转双层石墨烯中实现原子级精度的层间耦合调控，并发现小扭转角下摩尔相关边峰在约1.3 nm层间距时仍存在，表明层间能带杂化具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并调控范德华材料中的层间耦合对于设计关联态、拓扑相与超导等新奇电子现象至关重要；TBG可提供层间取向与间距影响量子输运和摩尔物理的可调平台，但缺乏对层间耦合的精确、原子级控制手段。

Method: 采用由超薄、厚度可调hBN间隔层分隔的双栅器件，对TBG层间耦合进行精确的原子级调控；在固定扭转角下系统改变层间距，并测量电子-空穴双层行为所需的临界位移场；结合层间距控制与独立扭转角可调性。

Result: 在固定扭转角下，电子-空穴双层行为所需的临界位移场随层间距增大而减小；在小扭转角下，即使层间被四层hBN（约1.3 nm）分隔，摩尔相关边峰仍然存在，说明层间能带杂化较强且稳健。

Conclusion: 该平台将层间耦合的原子层精度调控与独立扭转角调节相结合，为发现由工程化层间耦合与摩尔图案共同主导的新奇摩尔物理开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Understanding and controlling interlayer coupling in van der Waals (vdW) materials is crucial for engineering novel electronic phenomena, including correlated states, topological phases, and superconductivity. Twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) offers a highly tunable platform to explore how interlayer orientation and separation influence quantum transport and moiré physics. Here, we introduce a method for achieving precise, atomic-scale control of interlayer coupling in TBG using dual-gated devices separated by ultrathin, thickness-tunable hBN spacers. This approach enables systematic control of interlayer interactions, demonstrating that the critical displacement field required for electron-hole bilayer behavior decreases as the layer separation increases at a fixed twist angle. Remarkably, at small twist angles, moiré-related side peaks persist even when layers are separated by tetralayers of hBN (approximately 1.3 nm), indicating robust interlayer band hybridization. By combining atomic-layer-precise control of interlayer coupling with independent twist angle tunability, our platform opens new avenues for discovering novel moiré physics governed by engineered interlayer coupling and moiré patterns.

</details>


### [113] [Interlayer Fermi Polarons in Bilayer MoTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38045)
*Ruihao Ni,Eugen Dizer,Maximilian Wolf,Son T. Le,Sharadh Jois,Jeffrey J. Schwartz,Liuxin Gu,Rundong Ma,Suji Park,Beini Gao,Lifu Zhang,Houk Jang,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Aubrey T. Hanbicki,Adam L. Friedman,Richard Schmidt,You Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用具有直接光学带隙的双层MoTe₂平台，首次揭示了由层赝自旋主导的双层费米极化子物理，发现了一种单层中不存在的、由层相干激子被对侧载流子修饰形成的新型极化子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层过渡金属硫化物（TMDs）可承载由层自由度调控的量子相（如双层维格纳晶体、分数量子陈绝缘体、激子凝聚体），这些相主要通过激子谱学探测。然而，在双层体系中，激子与载流子如何相互作用形成费米极化子尚不清楚，尤其是当杂质与费米海均携带层赝自旋时。研究进展受限的主要原因在于多数TMD双层具有动量间接光学带隙，非辐射衰减和inhomogeneous broadening掩盖了本征光谱。

Method: 论文指出双层MoTe₂不同于多数TMD双层，保留了直接光学带隙，为研究双层费米极化子物理提供了干净平台。在双栅器件中，面外电场可连续调控层内激子与层间激子的杂化，形成层相干激子；在静电掺杂后，激子谱演化为多个极化子分支，受载流子掺杂和面外电场共同调控。作者结合场论模型对实验结果进行定量分析。

Result: 在静电掺杂下，激子谱演化出多个极化子分支，其行为由载流子掺杂与面外电场共同控制。其中识别出一种在单层中无对应物的极化子——层相干激子被相反层中的载流子修饰，并能被作者的场论模型定量描述。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了杂质与费米海双方的赝自旋结构会重塑极化子的形成，为探索具有层间相干性的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚等多体态开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Atomic bilayers of transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) host quantum phases governed by the layer degree of freedom, including bilayer Wigner crystals, fractional Chern insulators, and exciton condensates. These phases are probed primarily through exciton spectroscopy, yet it remains poorly understood how excitons and carriers interact to form Fermi polarons in bilayers, where both the impurity and the Fermi sea carry a layer pseudospin. Progress has been limited because most TMD bilayers have momentum-indirect optical bandgaps, in which non-radiative decay and inhomogeneous broadening obscure the intrinsic spectra. Here, we show that bilayer MoTe$_2$, unlike most TMD bilayers, retains a direct optical bandgap, providing a clean platform for studying bilayer Fermi-polaron physics. In a dual-gated device, an out-of-plane electric field continuously tunes the hybridization between intralayer and interlayer excitons, forming layer-coherent excitons. Upon electrostatic doping, the excitonic spectrum evolves into multiple polaron branches, controlled by both carrier doping and the out-of-plane electric field. Among these, we identify a polaron with no analog in monolayers, in which a layer-coherent exciton is dressed by carriers in the opposite layer, and is quantitatively captured by our field-theoretic model. Our results establish that pseudospin structure in both the impurity and the bath reshapes polaron formation, opening new avenues to many-body states such as Bose-Einstein condensates with interlayer coherence.

</details>


### [114] [Ohm's law for information revealed by a skyrmion Maxwell's demon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36955)
*Soma Miki,Yoshishige Suzuki,Ryo Ishikawa,Yuki Hibino,Shunya Morishita,Hiroto Imanishi,Keigo Adachi,Yoichi Shiota,Takayuki Nozaki,Minori Goto,Hikaru Nomura,Shigemi Mizukami,Eiiti Tamura*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在室温下利用两个做布朗运动的磁性斯格明子构建信息引擎，在较宽范围内验证了信息流与信息亲和力成正比，从而实验确立了“信息欧姆定律”，并指出实际信息电路中该定律与基尔霍夫电压定律需因载体相互作用及信息流散射而作微修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低计算能耗是AI时代亟待解决的问题，需要基于信息热力学的电路设计框架。非平衡统计力学扩展到信息热力学后，理论上预测信息流由其共轭热力学力（信息亲和力）驱动，且信息输运应遵循类似欧姆定律的线性响应关系，但该“类欧姆本构关系”此前从未被实验证实。

Method: 将非平衡统计力学推广至信息热力学以定义信息流与信息亲和力；在室温下构建由两个磁性斯格明子布朗运动组成的信息引擎，其中信息亲和力由麦克斯韦妖产生；通过测量信息流与信息亲和力的关系检验线性响应规律。

Result: 在较宽的参数范围内，测得信息流与麦克斯韦妖产生的信息亲和力成正比，实验确立了信息欧姆定律；同时发现在真实信息电路中，信息欧姆定律与基尔霍夫电压定律都需略微修正，以计入信息载体间的相互作用以及信息流的散射。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实验验证了信息输运的类欧姆线性本构关系，为基于信息热力学的电路设计提供了实验基础；并提示实际信息电路需在欧姆定律和基尔霍夫电压定律基础上引入相互作用与散射修正。

Abstract: Reducing the energy cost of computation-a pressing issue in the age of AI-requires a framework for circuit design based on information thermodynamics. By extending non-equilibrium statistical mechanics to information thermodynamics, it has been shown that a probability flow carrying information-hereinafter referred to as an 'information current'-is driven by its conjugate thermodynamic force, namely 'information affinity'. Since information affinity plays a role analogous to that of voltage in an electrical circuit, it is suggested that information transport also follows a linear response relationship analogous to Ohm's law. However, despite this theoretical prediction, experimentally establishing an Ohm-like constitutive relation for information transport remains an important unsolved problem. Here, we realise an information engine consisting of two magnetic skyrmions undergoing Brownian motion at room temperature. We demonstrate that the resulting information current is proportional to the information affinity generated by Maxwell's demon over a relatively wide range, establishing Ohm's law for information. Furthermore, we clarify that, in real information circuits, both Ohm's law for information and Kirchhoff's voltage law must be slightly modified to account for interactions between information carriers and the scattering of the information current.

</details>


### [115] [Deep Learning GW Quasiparticle Hamiltonians for Many-Body Excited-State Electronic Structure at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36962)
*Xiaoxun Gong,Zechen Tang,Woochang Kim,Yang Li,Wenhui Duan,Yong Xu,Steven G. Louie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出 DeepH-GW 深度学习框架，可直接从原子结构预测有效 GW 准粒子哈密顿量，实现线性标度、跨尺度可迁移且误差仅数 meV 的准粒子能带预测，并能捕捉 GW 级电声带隙重整化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准粒子电子结构是理解材料激发态性质及光电子、量子、输运现象的基础，但第一性原理 GW 计算对大体系或构型复杂体系仍计算量巨大，亟需兼顾平面波方法的系统精度/化学普适性与高效推理的新方案。

Method: DeepH-GW 基于 DeepH 的局域、等变消息传递架构，通过实空间哈密顿量重构接口，以高精度平面波 GW 计算为训练数据，学习从原子结构到有效 GW 准粒子哈密顿量的映射；推理阶段为线性标度神经网络。

Result: 在所考察体系中，DeepH-GW 复现准粒子能带的误差约为数 meV；尽管多体相互作用本质非局域，该框架仍展现从小训练结构到大超胞的强跨尺度迁移能力；并能在含热位移的超胞中准确捕捉 GW 级电声带隙重整化。

Conclusion: DeepH-GW 将平面波 GW 的系统精度与神经网络的线性标度推理相结合，为大尺度多体模拟及激发态电子结构基础模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Accurate quasiparticle electronic structures are the foundation for understanding excited-state properties of materials and explaining optoelectronic, quantum, and transport phenomena. First-principles GW calculations nevertheless remain computationally intensive for large or configurationally complex systems. Here we introduce DeepH-GW, a deep-learning framework that predicts an effective GW quasiparticle Hamiltonian directly from atomic structure. Building on the local, equivariant message-passing architecture of DeepH, DeepH-GW is trained on high-fidelity plane-wave GW calculations through a real-space Hamiltonian-reconstruction interface. This approach combines the systematic accuracy and broad chemical applicability of plane-wave methods with linear-scaling neural-network inference. Across the systems examined, DeepH-GW reproduces quasiparticle band structures with errors on the order of a few meV. Moreover, we show that, despite the intrinsic nonlocality of many-body interactions, DeepH-GW exhibits strong cross-scale transferability from relatively small training structures to substantially larger supercells. We demonstrate that the framework can accurately capture GW-level electron-phonon band-gap renormalization through predictions in supercells with thermal displacements, illustrating the power of the approach. DeepH-GW therefore provides a practical route toward large-scale many-body simulations and foundation models for excited-state electronic structure.

</details>


### [116] [Spin separation of Dirac electrons by twisted and vector optical beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37010)
*A. A. Gunyaga,M. V. Durnev,S. A. Tarasenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 扭曲光和矢量光束可由结构光诱导自旋流，实现二维电子中相反自旋投影的空间分离，并在准局域与扩散控制两种机制下导出解析式，针对TMD单层参数数值计算自旋织构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将光束的扭曲与矢量偏振图案"印刻"到电子自旋自由度的机制，实现自旋的空间分离与可控自旋分布。

Method: 利用结构光诱导的发散自旋流，结合自旋弛豫与扩散过程，推导准局域和扩散控制两种极限下的自旋密度解析表达式，并对过渡金属硫族化合物单层相关参数进行自旋织构的数值计算。

Result: 证明了扭曲光束与矢量光束能够空间分离自旋投影相反的二维电子，获得自旋密度解析解，并给出与TMD单层参数相符的自旋织构。

Conclusion: 提供了一种将光束的扭曲与矢量图案映射到电子自旋自由度上的机制，为光学调控二维材料自旋提供新途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that twisted and vector optical beams can spatially separate two-dimensional electrons with opposite spin projections. Spin accumulation arises from diverging spin currents induced by structured light together with spin relaxation and diffusion. We derive analytical expressions for the resulting spin density in the quasi-local and diffusion-controlled regimes and numerically calculate the spin textures for the parameters relevant to transition metal dichalcogenide monolayers. Our results suggest a mechanism by which the twist and vector pattern of optical beams can be imprinted onto the electron spin degree of freedom.

</details>


### [117] [Cavity-QED enhancement of quantum entanglement and battery performance in double quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37034)
*Hamid Arian Zad,Usama Shoukat,Michal Jaščur,Hazrat Ali,Saeed Haddadi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文理论研究单电子硅双量子点（eDQD）与单模微波腔耦合系统中的量子关联与能量存储，通过约化热密度矩阵用concurrence和$l_1$范数表征自旋-电荷纠缠与量子相干，发现腔诱导的eDQD主导区与光子缀饰区之间的非线性交叉，并将eDQD作为量子电池评估其储能与ergotropy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Rashba自旋轨道耦合（SOC）已有的研究基础上，探索微波腔耦合是否能作为额外的可调手段来控制量子关联与能量存储，并揭示腔缀饰效应对可提取功的影响。

Method: 构建单电子硅双量子点与单模微波腔耦合模型，电荷与自旋自由度由Rashba SOC杂化，腔通过自旋-光子与电荷-光子耦合与eDQD相互作用；利用约化热密度矩阵，以concurrence度量自旋-电荷纠缠，以$l_1$范数度量量子相干；并将系统作为量子电池在相干充电下评估其储能与ergotropy。

Result: 低温下腔强烈改变自旋-电荷关联，出现纠缠增强与抑制的明显区域；识别出eDQD主导区与光子缀饰区之间的非线性交叉，该交叉由concurrence剧变和腔内有限光子占据两个独立信号揭示，其边界呈$G_{\rm c}\propto\sqrt{\Omega}$主导依赖且对腔Hilbert空间截断稳健；ergotropy在类似缀饰交叉处显著变化，优化电荷-光子耦合可得到参数依赖的最优充电区，且随Rashba耦合增强最大ergotropy提高。

Conclusion: 腔耦合与SOC可作为半导体eDQD-腔系统中量子关联与可提取能量的互补控制手段。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate quantum correlations and energy storage in a single-electron silicon double quantum dot (eDQD) coupled to a single-mode microwave cavity. The charge and spin degrees of freedom are hybridized by Rashba spin-orbit coupling (SOC), while the cavity interacts with the eDQD through spin-photon and charge-photon couplings. Using the reduced thermal density matrix, we characterize spin-charge entanglement by concurrence and quantum coherence by the $l_1$ norm. At low temperature, the cavity strongly modifies the spin-charge correlations, producing distinct regimes of enhanced and suppressed entanglement. We identify a nonlinear crossover between an eDQD-dominated regime and a photon-dressed regime. This crossover is revealed independently by two signatures: a rapid change in the concurrence and the onset of finite photon occupation in the cavity. Its boundary exhibits a dominant $G_{\rm c}\propto\sqrtΩ$ dependence and is robust against cavity Hilbert-space truncation. Beyond the Rashba coupling studied in [Ferreira et al., Phys. Rev. A 107, 052408 (2023)], we show that the cavity provides an additional tunable means of controlling quantum correlations and energy storage. We further characterize the eDQD as a quantum battery under coherent charging and evaluate its stored energy and ergotropy. The ergotropy varies markedly across a cavity-dressing crossover resembling that identified from entanglement, connecting cavity-induced modification of the dressed states to extractable work. Optimization over the charge-photon coupling reveals parameter-dependent optimal charging regimes and enhanced maximum ergotropy with increasing Rashba coupling. Our results establish cavity coupling and SOC as complementary controls of quantum correlations and extractable energy in semiconductor eDQD-cavity systems.

</details>


### [118] [Symmetry-enforced topological parity: revisiting $2\mathbb{Z}$ classifications beyond cellwise symmetry actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37106)
*Ken Shiozaki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 动量依赖的对称性作用可将2Z分类的偶数约束变为奇数约束，本文推导了Chern/绕数与对称矩阵拓扑不变量的宇称关系，并给出奇数拓扑数的可实现性分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑绝缘体与超导体的2Z分类通常只保证偶数拓扑数（即允许拓扑平凡相）。作者希望研究在不改变对称代数的情况下，动量依赖的对称性作用如何将偶数约束转化为奇数约束，从而使任何保对称的能隙相必然拓扑非平凡——这为强拓扑相的构造提供新机制。

Method: 针对内部对称性与二阶晶格对称性，在至多四个空间维度中，推导能隙哈密顿量的Chern数或绕数与动量依赖反酉对称矩阵的拓扑不变量之间的宇称关系；以cellwise作用（固定单胞基下动量无关矩阵）为参照，分析局域性如何限制奇数拓扑数的实现；结合显式模型构造与no-go定理进行分类。

Result: 当对称作用强制奇数宇称时，任何保对称能隙相必拓扑非平凡。奇数拓扑数分为三种情形：(1)可由有限程（finite-range）对称作用实现；(2)可由指数衰减的准局域（quasilocal）作用实现，但不可由有限程作用实现；(3)在有限维Bloch系统中不可能实现。

Conclusion: 动量依赖对称作用提供了一种将2Z分类提升为奇数约束的机制，并给出了奇数拓扑数可行性的完整三分类，揭示了局域性对拓扑相实现的根本限制。

Abstract: Momentum-dependent symmetry actions can turn the even-integer constraint of a $2\mathbb{Z}$ classification of topological insulators and superconductors into an odd-integer constraint without changing the symmetry algebra. For internal and order-two crystalline symmetries in up to four spatial dimensions, we derive parity relations between the Chern or winding number of a gapped Hamiltonian and topological invariants of its momentum-dependent antiunitary symmetry matrices. Whenever the symmetry action enforces odd parity, any symmetry-preserving gapped phase must be topologically nontrivial. Using cellwise actions, which are represented by momentum-independent matrices in a fixed unit-cell basis, as a reference, we determine how locality constrains the realization of odd topological numbers. Explicit model constructions and no-go theorems distinguish three cases: odd values can be realized by finite-range symmetry actions, can be realized by exponentially decaying quasilocal actions but not by finite-range actions, or are impossible in finite-dimensional Bloch systems.

</details>


### [119] [Engineering Vacuum Fluctuations in Hyperbolic Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37182)
*Jie-Cheng Feng,Xinle Cheng,Itai Keren,Dante M. Kennes,Abhay N. Pasupathy,Dmitri N. Basov,Emil Vinas Bostrom,Angel Rubio*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过在双曲材料上覆盖一层更薄、具有相反双曲性（相反符号介电张量）的覆盖层，可在不改变底层材料本征介电性质的情况下重塑其内部真空场，进而远程调控其内部物质激发与超导临界温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用光学腔、谐振器和表面极化激元模式可以工程化真空涨落，但将这类真空场调控延伸到材料内部仍具挑战。本文旨在寻求一种在材料深处实现真空场重塑与远程调控物质激发的方案。

Method: 采用双曲材料与具有相反双曲性的更薄覆盖层构成异质结构；要求两层双曲频带存在重叠，从而重塑底层材料内部的真空场并改变真空场与底层物质激发的耦合；以Eliashberg理论分析底层材料中二维超导层的行为，计算覆盖层对其临界温度的影响。

Result: 覆盖层能够在不改变底层材料本征介电性质的前提下调控其内部真空场，并远程改变底层二维超导层的临界温度；该机制可解释实验中频率匹配的双曲覆盖层引起的超导性变化。

Conclusion: 双曲覆盖层可通过频带重叠重塑底层材料内部的真空场，实现对物质激发与集体材料性质的远程、片上调控，为长程调控物质特性提供了新途径。

Abstract: Vacuum fluctuations can be engineered using optical cavities, resonators, and surface-polaritonic modes, but extending such control deep inside a material remains challenging.Here we show that the vacuum field inside a hyperbolic material can be reshaped by a much thinner overlayer with opposite hyperbolicity.This effect requires overlap between the hyperbolic bands of the two materials and modifies the coupling between the vacuum field and matter excitations within the bottom material, without changing its intrinsic dielectric properties. As an application, we use Eliashberg theory to study a two-dimensional superconducting layer inside the bottom material, showing that the overlayer can remotely change its critical temperature.Our results may shed light on recent experiments reporting changes in superconductivity arising from a frequency-matched hyperbolic overlayer, and suggest an on-chip route to long-range control of matter excitations and collective material properties.

</details>


### [120] [Atomic-scale control of spin transport in exchange-coupled molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37299)
*A. Fétida,R. Robles,F. Scheurer,M. Romeo,J. Kröger,N. Lorente,L. Limot*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过在扫描隧道显微镜针尖上功能化单分子作为可移动自旋传感器，在铁磁表面上的第二个分子上方构建交换耦合分子结，从同一光谱数据集独立提取电导、交换耦合和自旋极化，并揭示其空间关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋输运由隧穿和交换相互作用共同支配，但二者在真实空间中的微观关系此前一直缺乏实验证据。

Method: 将单个分子功能化到扫描隧道显微镜针尖上，作为可移动自旋传感器置于铁磁表面上另一分子上方，形成交换耦合分子结；从同一光谱数据集独立提取结电导、交换耦合和自旋极化，并获得原子级空间分辨。

Result: 电导与交换耦合表现出几乎相同的空间依赖关系，符合最小Hubbard描述，即两者由分子前沿轨道间相同的跃迁矩阵支配；而自旋极化则取决于轨道组成及由此产生的输运通道相对权重。

Conclusion: 这些结果在磁耦合分子中建立了隧穿、交换与自旋输运之间的真实空间联系。

Abstract: Spin transport between coupled spins is governed by tunneling and exchange interactions, yet their microscopic relation in real space has remained experimentally elusive. To address this, we functionalize the tip of a scanning tunneling microscope with a single molecule and use it as a movable spin sensor positioned above a second molecule on a ferromagnetic surface, forming an exchange-coupled molecular junction. From the same spectroscopic dataset, we independently extract the junction conductance, exchange coupling, and spin polarization with atomic-scale resolution. Conductance and exchange display nearly identical spatial dependences, consistent with a minimal Hubbard description in which both are governed by the same hopping matrix between the molecular frontier orbitals. In contrast, the spin polarization depends on the orbital composition and the relative weight of the resulting transport channels. These results establish a real-space connection between tunneling, exchange, and spin transport in magnetically coupled molecules.

</details>


### [121] [Nonlinear optical responses of semiconducting graphene nanoribbons: Strong constraint from a hidden symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37418)
*Jiawei Ruan,Weichen Tang,Yang-Hao Chan,Chen Hu,Xiaoxun Gong,Steven G. Louie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文指出石墨烯纳米带中近似的二分晶格粒子-空穴（BLPH）对称性可强烈抑制偶数阶非线性光学效应，即使体系无反演对称性；据此在铁磁GNR中实现近乎纯自旋位移电流的光学产生，且自旋取向可由外磁场调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知中偶数阶非线性光学效应主要由空间反演对称性禁止，但非空间对称性对NLO响应的约束被忽视；同时，在反铁磁体系中产生纯自旋位移电流的方案存在自旋取向调控困难等问题，需要新的对称性机制与材料平台。

Method: 基于具有二分晶格结构的石墨烯纳米带（GNRs），识别并利用近似的二分晶格粒子-空穴（BLPH）对称性来分析偶数阶NLO响应；结合铁磁GNR体系计算电荷位移电流与自旋位移电流，并以近期合成的铁磁Janus GNR为实例进行验证。

Result: BLPH对称性显著抑制偶数阶NLO效应，即便体系不具备反演对称性；在铁磁GNR中实现光学产生的近纯自旋位移电流，电荷位移电流被强烈抑制；该光致直流自旋电流的自旋取向可通过外磁场便捷调控，优于以往反铁磁体系方案。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了NLO响应中被忽视的对称性维度，强调非空间对称性对光学效应的约束作用，并将磁性石墨烯纳米带定位为自旋电子学器件的有前景候选材料。

Abstract: Nonlinear optical (NLO) responses are powerful tools for probing material structures, as they are highly sensitive to spatial symmetries. For instance, it is well known that having inversion symmetry forbids even-order NLO effects. Here, we show that nonspatial symmetries can also significantly constrain NLO responses. In graphene nanoribbons (GNRs) with bipartite-lattice structures, we identify the approximate bipartite-lattice particle-hole (BLPH) symmetry strongly suppresses even-order NLO effects, even for systems with no inversion symmetry. Importantly, we demonstrate that this approximate symmetry enables the optical generation of nearly pure spin shift currents in ferromagnetic GNRs, with the charge shift currents strongly suppressed. The spin orientation of these photo-induced DC spin currents can be easily tuned by an external magnetic field, providing distinct advantages over previous schemes for generating pure spin shift currents in antiferromagnetic systems. We illustrate our findings using a recently synthesized ferromagnetic Janus GNR. Our results underscore the significance of an overlooked symmetry aspect of NLO responses and highlight magnetic GNRs as promising candidates for spintronic devices.

</details>


### [122] [Rotational symmetry and common-mode phase drift in a counter-wound S-shaped Aharonov--Bohm interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37753)
*Halil Serdar Solak,Afif Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究三维InAs Aharonov-Bohm波导中两个反向弯曲臂的共模磁通偏移问题，证明微可逆性和模块交换旋转对称导致透射率满足T(φ1,φ2)=T(-φ2,-φ1)，从而使反向绕制结构对共模偏移不敏感，并给出三维散射数值估计及噪声响应分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在固体Aharonov-Bohm波导中利用对称性抑制共模磁通偏移（如环境均匀磁场）对量子输运的干扰。

Method: 基于自旋无关双端模型，利用理想约束磁通下的微可逆性及交换完整模块的旋转对称性进行解析推导；在R=350 nm、EF≈7.283 meV条件下进行三维散射计算，保留三个开放轨道铅模；并分析弱共模高斯相位噪声下的透射方差与均值移动。

Result: 反向绕制配置对共模偏移稳定，同向绕制对差分偏移稳定；最细采样的反向绕制扫掠可见度约3.56%，参考共同阈值估计连续可见度约2.6–2.9%；响应系数随能量变化强烈，100 mK电子温度保留约98%可见度；弱共模高斯相位噪声下透射方差为噪声幅度的四次方项，均值移动为二次项；磁通不平衡和非对称标量势恢复线性响应，对称标量无序保持稳定性但不保证高透射。

Conclusion: 该对称性约束不需要所有干涉相位相互抵消，并可推广到垂直波导平面的空间均匀环境磁场；实验验证需要少模相干输运、校准磁通控制及对泄漏场和自旋相关项进行评估的磁源设计。

Abstract: We study common flux offsets in a three-dimensional InAs Aharonov-Bohm waveguide formed by two quarter-circle bends of opposite curvature. Each bend contains a through-opening that separates two conducting arms and encloses an independently specified flux. For a spin-independent, two-terminal model with ideal confined fluxes, microreversibility and a rotation exchanging the complete modules imply $\mathcal{T}(φ_1,φ_2)=\mathcal{T}(-φ_2,-φ_1)$. The counter-wound configuration is therefore stationary against common offsets, while the same-winding configuration is stationary against differential offsets. This constraint does not require cancellation of every interference phase, and it extends to a spatially uniform ambient field perpendicular to the plane of the guide, even though that field also penetrates the conductor. Three-dimensional scattering calculations at $R=350$ nm and $E_F\simeq 7.283$ meV retain three open orbital lead modes and strong reflection at the openings. The finest sampled counter-wound sweep has a visibility of approximately 3.56%; referring the grids to a common threshold gives an estimated continuum visibility of 2.6-2.9%. The response coefficients vary strongly with energy, while a 100 mK electron temperature retains about 98% of the visibility. For weak, purely common Gaussian phase noise, the leading transmission variance is quartic in noise amplitude, whereas the mean shift remains quadratic. Flux imbalance and asymmetric scalar potentials restore a linear response; symmetric scalar disorder preserves stationarity without guaranteeing high transmission. The proposed experimental test requires few-mode coherent transport, calibrated flux controls, and a magnetic-source design whose leakage fields and spin-dependent terms are assessed explicitly.

</details>


### [123] [Multistate Torsion in Zitterbewegung](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37767)
*Wojciech J. Jankowski*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将晶格能带扭转引入电子运动方程，推导出非线性Zitterbewegung速度，预测了区别于圆偏振光所致稳态光伏效应的瞬态非线性光电流，并提出多态量子几何扭转张量的层析测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验观测到圆偏振光诱导的共振稳态光伏效应，但其与能带几何（尤其多态量子几何与扭转）在非平衡瞬态过程中的作用尚不清晰；需要在理论上厘清带扭转对电子本征运动（Zitterbewegung）及光电流响应的影响。

Method: 基于多态量子几何张量与带扭转构建电子运动方程，推导非线性Zitterbewegung速度；结合非平衡瞬态求解得到非线性光电流响应，并设计用于测量多态量子几何扭转张量的层析（tomography）协议。

Result: 理论给出由带扭转增强的非线性Zitterbewegung速度，并得到瞬态非线性光电流，其响应特征显著区别于已测量的圆偏振光共振稳态光伏效应；同时提出可操作的多态量子几何扭转张量层析测量方案。

Conclusion: 带扭转是多态量子几何驱动的非线性电子动力学与瞬态光电流的关键来源，为区分稳态与瞬态光电流机制提供了判据，并为多态量子几何扭转张量的实验测量提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We derive nonlinear Zitterbewegung velocity of electrons augmented by band torsion in crystals. The equation of motion with multistate quantum geometric tensors and torsion culminates in transient nonlinear photocurrent responses distinct from the recently measured resonant steady-state photovoltaic effects induced by circularly polarized light. Tomography protocols for measuring the multistate quantum geometric torsion tensors are proposed.

</details>


### [124] [Multiscale modelling of nanoscaled FETs based on 2D ferroelectric materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37823)
*Alejandro Toral-Lopez,Michele Virgilio,Gianluca Fiori,Damiano Marian*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个连接第一性原理DFT与NEGF输运计算的多尺度仿真框架，以二维InP单层为例，通过连续插值的极化依赖哈密顿量嵌入自洽Poisson-NEGF求解器，无需经验参数即可复现宏观电滞回线与存储窗口，为下一代二维铁电场效应晶体管的评估与优化提供高效预测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电材料在低功耗、高速电子学及神经形态计算中极具吸引力，但传统三维块体铁电材料在纳米尺度存在严重物理缩放限制，促使研究转向二维铁电单层。加速这类新材料的实验测试与筛选，亟需准确的器件级性能预测方法。

Method: 构建多尺度仿真框架，将第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）与非平衡格林函数（NEGF）输运计算相桥接。以二维磷化铟（InP）单层为案例，采用连续插值、依赖极化的哈密顿量，并将其嵌入自洽Poisson-NEGF求解器中，以刻画离子运动与电子输运之间的动态耦合。

Result: 该模型能够自然复现宏观电滞回线与存储窗口，且无需引入任何经验参数；同时可高效捕捉离子迁移与电子输运的相互作用。

Conclusion: 该预测性流程为评估和优化下一代二维铁电场效应晶体管（2D FeFET）提供了一种计算高效的工具，有助于加速新型二维铁电材料的筛选与器件设计。

Abstract: Ferroelectric materials are highly attractive for low-power, high-speed electronics and emerging neuromorphic computing architectures. However, the severe physical scaling limits of conventional bulk (3D) ferroelectrics at the nanoscale have shifted attention towards two-dimensional (2D) ferroelectric monolayers. Accurate device-level performance predictions are essential to accelerate the experimental testing and screening of these novel materials. In this work, we present a multiscale simulation framework that bridges first-principles density functional theory with Non-Equilibrium Green's Function (NEGF) transport calculations. Using a 2D Indium Phosphide monolayer as a case study, our approach leverages a continuously interpolated, polarization-dependent Hamiltonian embedded within a self-consistent Poisson-NEGF solver. The model captures the dynamic interplay between ion movement and electronic transport, naturally reproducing macroscopic hysteresis loops and memory windows without empirical parameters. This predictive pipeline provides a computationally efficient tool to evaluate and optimize next-generation 2D ferroelectric field-effect transistors.

</details>


### [125] [Interlayer dark excitons in a van der Waals heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38033)
*Rundong Ma,Konstantin Davydov,Liuxin Gu,Lifu Zhang,Hassan Alnatah,Beini Gao,Ruihao Ni,Suji Park,Houk Jang,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,You Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用双栅WSe₂/hBN/WSe₂同质双层，通过电场依赖的PL与反射光谱，直接区分了亮（单重态）与暗（三重态）层间激子，并揭示暗层间激子具有超长寿命（>微秒）与强谷极化，且可通过扭转角调控其发射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMD异质结中的层间激子具有长寿命与长程输运特性，是实现激子器件与激子多体相（如BEC）的候选平台。决定粒子布居、输运与凝聚行为的是最低能激子态，因此必须准确理解自旋允许的亮激子与名义自旋禁戒的暗激子。然而，异质结中的莫尔激子可模仿其光学特征，导致单重态与三重态层间激子难以被明确区分。

Method: 样品为高质量、双栅调控的WSe₂/hBN/WSe₂同质双层。采用电场依赖的光致发光（PL）与反射（reflectance）光谱进行测量，识别动量直接（momentum-direct）的层间跃迁，并结合其自旋构型与选择规则进行判定；同时通过改变扭转角引入电子-空穴动量失配，考察其对层间激子发射的调制。

Result: 实验直接识别出两种动量直接的层间跃迁，分别对应亮（单重态）与暗（三重态）层间激子，且二者自旋构型不同。暗层间激子遵循与亮激子不同的选择规则。暗层间激子保持强谷极化，同时具有超过微秒的超长寿命。扭转角可调控层间激子发射：扭转导致的电子-空穴动量失配可调制该发射。

Conclusion: 该研究实现了对TMD同质双层中亮/暗层间激子的直接光谱区分，厘清了其自旋、选择规则与谷极化行为，并证明了扭转角作为调控自由度的作用，为理解TMD异质结的电子与激子结构提供了关键信息，为激子多体物理与光电器件开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Interlayer excitons in transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) heterostructures exhibit long lifetimes and long-range transport, making them promising for excitonic devices and quantum many-body phases, such as Bose-Einstein condensates. Achieving these goals requires a precise understanding of spin-allowed bright and nominally spin-forbidden dark excitons, because the lowest-energy exciton species governs population, transport, and condensation. Despite substantial progress, unambiguously distinguishing singlet and triplet interlayer excitons has been challenging, as moiré excitons in these heterostructures can mimic their optical signatures. Here, we report the direct spectroscopic identification of bright (singlet) and dark (triplet) interlayer excitons in high-quality, dual-gated WSe$_2$/hBN/WSe$_2$ homobilayers. Electric-field-dependent photoluminescence and reflectance reveal two momentum-direct interlayer transitions with distinct spin configurations. The interlayer dark excitons obey selection rules that differ from those of bright excitons. Strikingly, interlayer dark excitons retain strong valley polarization, even with their ultralong lifetime exceeding microseconds. Finally, we demonstrate twist-angle control, wherein twist-induced electron-hole momentum mismatch modulates interlayer exciton emission. These results provide critical insights into the electronic and excitonic structure of TMD heterostructures, opening new avenues for excitonic many-body physics and optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [126] [Spatially dispersive photogalvanic effects as a probe of quantum geometric tensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38102)
*Yicong Chen,Bumseop Kim,Senin Ahammed Akkara Paramban,Zhurun Ji,Utkarsh Khandelwal,Shupeng Xu,Jiachen Shi,Sergiy Krylyuk,Albert V. Davydov,Andrew M. Rappe,Ritesh Agarwal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出利用光的空间色散（spatial dispersion）改变二阶光电流的选择定则，使中心对称晶体无需任何外场或对称性破缺即可产生本征响应，并在1T'-MoTe2中分别提取量子度规与Berry曲率贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子度规和Berry曲率共同支配大量Bloch态相关现象，但目前缺乏统一、直接的实验手段同时获取二者；传统光电流效应（如位移电流）受晶体对称性限制，且将量子度规与shift vector贡献纠缠在一起，难以分离。

Method: 利用光场的空间色散（波矢梯度）引入新的光电流选择定则，使原本被宇称禁戒的二阶响应在中心对称晶体中成为允许，且响应权重由Bloch态的量子几何决定；通过不同偏振通道分离量子度规与Berry曲率贡献，并在1T'-MoTe2（温度驱动向Td-Weyl相转变）器件上测量螺旋性偶（helicity-even）与螺旋性奇（helicity-odd）光电流，结合第一性原理计算对照。

Result: 在1T'-MoTe2中，同一器件上同时分辨出对应量子度规的helicity-even光电流和对应Berry曲率的helicity-odd光电流；度规主导响应在两种相中均存在，具有稳定的谱结构，可被第一性原理计算复现并关联到动量分辨的量子度规热点；曲率驱动通道仅在反演对称性破缺后出现，并通过竞争的动量空间贡献对载流子掺杂高度敏感。

Conclusion: 空间变化光场驱动的光电流（光生伏打）效应可作为普适途径，在光子能量与电子填充探测激发流形不同部分的材料中获取量子几何信息，实现了对量子度规和Berry曲率的统一且可分离的实验探测。

Abstract: Berry curvature and quantum metric of Bloch states govern a wide range of phenomena, yet unified experimental access to both remains limited. Here we show that light spatial-dispersion alters the selection rules of second-order photocurrents and responses forbidden by parity become allowed in centrosymmetric crystals without any external fields, magnetic order, interfaces, strain, or engineered symmetry breaking. The resulting response is intrinsic and its weight is set by the quantum geometry of the Bloch states. Conventional photogalvanic effects are constrained by crystal symmetry and entangle the quantum metric with shift vector contributions. The spatially-dispersive response instead isolates the quantum metric and Berry curvature in distinct polarization channels. Implementing this approach in 1T'-MoTe2 across its temperature-driven transition to Td-Weyl phase, we resolve helicity-even and helicity-odd photocurrents corresponding to quantum metric and Berry curvature contributions, respectively, from the same device. The metric-dominated response persists across both phases and exhibits a robust spectral structure reproduced by first-principles calculations and linked to momentum-resolved quantum metric hotspots. In contrast, the curvature-driven channel emerges only when inversion symmetry is broken and shows strong sensitivity to carrier doping through competing momentum-space contributions. Our results establish photogalvanic effects with spatially varying optical fields as a general route to accessing quantum geometry in materials where photon energy and electronic filling probe different parts of the excitation manifold.

</details>
