### [127] [Discrete Scale Invariance of Ising Mesons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35940)
*Denis V. Vasilyev,Abbas Ali Saberi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文证明离散标度不变性可在有序量子磁体内部激发中涌现：在边际1/r⁴横场Ising链中，长程键生成装扮畴壁间的渐近平方反比吸引，由自发磁化强度固定，其强度与双扭结阈值曲率共同决定标度异常指数，进而支配介子层级及几何/对数/散射标度，数值计算支持能—尺寸耦合标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散标度不变性通常源于共振少体动力学或奇异标度不变动力学，作者希望探寻其是否可由完全不同的机制——有序量子磁体内复合激发的内部运动——产生，从而为量子极限环物理提供微观多体实现。

Method: 系统为边际1/r⁴长程横场Ising链；利用长程键诱导的装扮畴壁间渐近平方反比吸引，其强度由自发磁化强度固定；结合装扮双扭结阈值曲率设定相对动能标度；采用全自旋精确对角化与四阶弱场哈密顿量进行数值验证。

Result: 两个独立可测量量（平方反比吸引强度与双扭结阈值曲率）共同确定标度异常指数，进而确定介子层级；在超临界区，同一指数支配几何束缚能与尺寸比、对数能级累积以及对数周期阈值散射；全自旋精确对角化与四阶弱场哈密顿量支持核心效应与环效应之间有限窗口内的能量—尺寸耦合标度。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种量子极限环物理的微观多体实现，其普适标度由磁序与扭结迁移率固定，表明离散标度不变性可从有序磁体内部激发的运动涌现。

Abstract: Discrete scale invariance often arises from resonant few-body or singular scale-invariant dynamics. We show that it can instead emerge in the internal motion of a composite excitation in an ordered quantum magnet. In the marginal $1/r^4$ transverse-field Ising chain, long-range bonds generate an asymptotic inverse-square attraction between dressed domain walls, with strength fixed by the spontaneous magnetization. The dressed two-kink threshold curvature sets the relative kinetic scale. Together, these independently accessible quantities determine the scale-anomaly exponent and hence the meson hierarchy. In the supercritical regime, the same exponent governs geometric binding-energy and size ratios, logarithmic level accumulation, and log-periodic threshold scattering. Full-spin exact diagonalization and a fourth-order weak-field Hamiltonian support the coupled energy--size scaling in the finite window between core and ring effects. This provides a microscopic many-body realization of quantum limit-cycle physics, with its universal scaling fixed by magnetic order and kink mobility.

</details>


### [128] [Locking transition in coupled disordered systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36846)
*Guy Bunin*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究沿链耦合、共享同一无序实现的多个随机能量模型（REM）副本：系统发生一级相变，从短程关联相进入"锁定相"，此时整条链在正温度下也以概率1占据最低能态，不存在中间关联长度；广义REM中链可锁定到最低能态或最低自由能谷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多组分相互作用系统在空间中延展时，各区域具有相同的无序环境，远端区域是否收敛到同一状态尚不清楚，因此需要研究共享无序的耦合副本体系。

Method: 构建沿链耦合、共享同一无序实现的REM副本模型，并进一步推广至广义REM（Generalized REM），分析其相变与锁定行为。

Result: 发现一级相变，将短程关联相与锁定相分开；在锁定相中整条链以概率1处于最低能态（即使在正温度下）；不存在中间有序长度；广义REM中链锁定于最低能态或最低自由能谷。

Conclusion: 共享无序的耦合REM副本表现出全有或全无的锁定相变，缺乏中间关联尺度，广义情形下锁定目标为最低能态或最低自由能谷。

Abstract: When a system of many interacting constituents is extended across space, every region carries the same disordered landscape, and it is unclear whether distant regions settle into the same state. We study copies of the Random Energy Model (REM), sharing one disorder realization, coupled along a chain. A first-order transition separates a phase with short-range correlations, from a locked phase in which the entire chain occupies the lowest-energy state with probability one, even at positive temperature. No intermediate ordering lengths occur. In the Generalized REM the chain locks either to the lowest-energy state or to the lowest free-energy valley.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Memory-induced optimal switching in a dynamic Ising-Kuramoto model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35838)
*Reon Hayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究自旋与相位全局耦合的随机系统，推导复合相干模式的同步阈值，发现切换速率存在使阈值最小化的内点最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有相干动力学研究多单独考虑相位同步或自旋序，缺乏对自旋翻转动力学与含记忆的相位噪声相互竞争如何影响复合相干模式阈值的系统分析。

Method: 对非磁非相干态做线性响应分析，引入记忆加权的自由相位关联函数拉普拉斯变换，推导同步阈值及内点最优的一般充分条件，并用有限尺寸模拟验证。

Result: 得到阈值K_c=[(μ+β_sγ)χ_γ]^{-1}；Glauber响应增强与记忆极化率下降的竞争导致阈值在有限切换速率处最小；有限时间OU关联满足所需的快切换渐近，白噪声极限则未必；模拟重现了非单调阈值、τ增大使最优向慢切换移动、非磁态反相复合序及磁化背景上的模式混合。

Conclusion: 自旋翻转与含记忆相位噪声的竞争可在有限切换速率处产生最优同步，对无白噪声极限的复合相干模式具有一般意义。

Abstract: We study a globally coupled stochastic system in which each unit carries an Ising spin and a continuous phase. The spins undergo Glauber switching with relaxation rate $γ$, while the phases are driven by Ornstein-Uhlenbeck noise with correlation time $τ$. A spin-dependent phase interaction supports a composite coherent mode, $Z_m=N^{-1}\sum_jσ_j e^{iθ_j}$, that can order even when the ordinary phase coherence is small. Linear response about the nonmagnetic incoherent state gives the synchronization threshold $K_{\mathrm c}=[(μ+β_sγ)χ_γ]^{-1}$, where $χ_γ$ is a memory-weighted Laplace transform of the free phase correlation function. The competition between the increasing Glauber response and the decreasing memory susceptibility produces a finite switching rate at which $K_{\mathrm c}$ is minimized. We derive a general sufficient condition for such an interior optimum and show that finite-time Ornstein-Uhlenbeck correlations satisfy the required fast-switching asymptotics, whereas the white-noise limit need not. Finite-size simulations reproduce the nonmonotonic threshold, its shift toward slower switching as $τ$ increases, antiphase composite order in a nonmagnetic state, and mode mixing on a magnetized background.

</details>


### [130] [Nonreciprocal Chiral Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35984)
*Andrew A. Allocca,Armin Rahmani,Pouyan Ghaemi,Sriram Ganeshan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于FHP-II的格子气元胞自动机模型，通过手性碰撞产生非零奇压力系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇压力是一种宇称奇输运系数，可实现非互易流体力学响应，但实现该系数的微观模型较为稀缺。

Method: 在含静止粒子的FHP-II模型基础上引入手性碰撞，即局部破缺宇称的碰撞使运动粒子在静止粒子存在时发生微弱旋转，再通过Chapman-Enskog粗粒化求解。

Result: 粗粒化得到非零奇压力系数；该微观规则在流体力学尺度上等效为有效磁场，将体粘度转化为奇压力，并推导了霍尔粘度、奇压力、奇力矩与宇称偶对应量之间的关系。

Conclusion: 手性碰撞的格子气模型可微观实现奇压力，并揭示其与其他宇称奇输运系数的联系。

Abstract: Odd pressure is a parity-odd transport coefficient that gives an isotropic pressure response to vorticity in compressible two-dimensional fluids. By asymmetrically coupling vortical and compressional modes, it provides a direct mechanism for a nonreciprocal hydrodynamic response reminiscent of several biological systems. Yet microscopic models realizing odd pressure as a transport coefficient are relatively scarce. Here, we construct one such model using a lattice-gas cellular automaton based on the Frisch-Hasslacher-Pomeau II model with rest particles and chiral collisions, whose Chapman-Enskog coarse-graining produces a nonzero odd pressure coefficient. The key ingredient is a local parity-breaking collision that weakly rotates moving particles in the presence of a rest particle. At hydrodynamic scales, this microscopic rule acts as an effective magnetic field, converting the model's bulk viscosity into odd pressure. More generally, we derive expressions relating Hall viscosity, odd pressure, and odd torque to their parity-even counterparts in our model.

</details>


### [131] [Autonomous self-harmonic drift in Langevin dynamics causes a compaction of underlying domain for the density of Stochastic Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36232)
*Ken Sekimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 将自谐漂移Langevin动力学与随机局部化对比，指出前者是后者在n维球面上的紧致化版本，并论证该紧致化源于要求漂移项自治，从而拓展随机局部化的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者近期研究自谐漂移与经典自旋的Langevin动力学时，发现其与数学几何及数据科学中受关注的"随机局部化"概念存在强关联，希望厘清两者关系并拓展随机局部化的研究范畴。

Method: 通过对比两个框架：随机局部化的概率分布函数定义域为n维欧氏空间，而所研究的Langevin动力学将其"紧致化"为n维球面，据此分析差异来源。

Result: 论证了从欧氏空间到n维球面的紧致化，是要求漂移项为自治（autonomous）所带来的结果。

Conclusion: 自谐漂移Langevin动力学可视为随机局部化在球面上的实现，两者关系有望扩展随机局部化的应用范围。

Abstract: Our recent research on Langevin dynamics with self-harmonic drift and classical spins has revealed a strong connection to ``Stochastic Localization," a concept attracting attention in the fields of mathematical geometry and data science. Roughly speaking, the former can be viewed as a version of the latter in which the $n$-dimensional Euclidean space - the domain of the probability distribution function appearing in the latter - is ``compactified'' to the surface of an $n$-dimensional sphere. By comparing the two frameworks, we argue that this compactification is a consequence of requiring the drift to be autonomous. The relationship between the two approaches may expand the scope of Stochastic Localization.

</details>


### [132] [Derivation of Fokker-Planck equation and its entropy production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36260)
*Tânia Tomé,Mário J. de Oliveira*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从主方程出发，在离散状态空间引入含常规项与扩散项的跃迁速率，于连续极限下推导出Fokker-Planck方程、熵产生率与熵流，并讨论了接触多个热库及孤立系统情形下的相应方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望从更基本的主方程（离散状态空间）出发，系统地导出Fokker-Planck方程及其熵产生，从而建立微观主方程与非平衡热力学熵产生描述之间的联系。

Method: 在离散状态空间中定义主方程，并引入由两部分构成的跃迁速率：一部分为与间距倒数成正比的常规项，另一部分与间距平方成反比、对应独立随机变量的方差；随后取连续空间极限进行推导。

Result: 在连续空间极限下得到了Fokker-Planck方程、熵产生率以及熵流；进一步针对状态空间为相空间的情形，给出了适用于与多个热库接触的系统以及孤立系统的相应方程。

Conclusion: 该工作表明可由主方程统一导出Fokker-Planck方程及熵产生与熵流，并可推广至相空间，涵盖多热库接触系统与孤立系统。

Abstract: We derive the Fokker-Planck equation and its entropy production from the master equation. To this end we consider a discrete space of states where the master equation is defined and appropriate transition rates are introduced. A transition rate has two parts, one of which is the ordinary term proportional to the inverse of the spacing and the other is inversely proportional to the square of the spacing corresponding to the variance of the independent stochastic variable. In the continuous space limit, we obtain the Fokker-Planck as well as the rate of entropy production and the flux of entropy. We also consider the case where the space of states is the phase space and discuss the equations appropriate for systems in contact with several heat reservoirs and for isolated systems.

</details>


### [133] [Localization Transition in Kinetically Deformed one-dimensional Aubry-André Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36446)
*Arpita Goswami*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出动能项的$q$-形变，构造厄米$q$-形变动能算符，破坏一维Aubry-André模型的精确自对偶性，从而改变其局域化结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AA模型在$\lambda_c=2$处所有本征态同时发生局域化转变，缺乏可调的中间相；作者希望通过引入单一参数控制的形变，实现可控地破缺精确自对偶性，从而调控局域化结构与相变行为。

Method: 将单粒子动能作为晶格平移算符的非线性函数进行$q$-形变，构造厄米$q$-形变动能算符，保持晶格均匀性并在$q\to1$极限回到常规AA哈密顿量。该形变产生由单参数$q$控制的无限组关联奇程跃迁过程，而非唯象引入长程跃迁。利用双变换分析该长程跃迁与对偶准周期势高次谐波的关系，并给出周期驱动AA模型的实验实现方案。

Result: $q\neq1$时精确自对偶性被破坏。与所有本征态在$\lambda_c=2$同时局域化的传统AA模型不同，形变模型在$\lambda_c=2$之外仍有部分本征态保持退局域化；在$q-\lambda$平面出现局域态与扩展态在整个谱内共存的中间区域。

Conclusion: $q$-形变动能提供了一种以$q$为控制参数、系统性地破缺AA模型自对偶性的机制，导致部分退局域态及局域-扩展共存中间相的出现，并可通过周期驱动AA模型实现。

Abstract: We propose a $q$-deformation in the single-particle kinetic energy and investigate how it modifies the localization in the one-dimensional Aubry-André (AA) model. We construct a Hermitian $q$-deformed kinetic operator as a nonlinear function of the lattice translation operator, preserving the uniform lattice and recovering the conventional AA Hamiltonian continuously in the undeformed limit $q\to1$. The deformation generates an infinite set of correlated odd-range kinetic processes, controlled by a single parameter q, rather than phenomenologically involving long-range hopping. Under the dual transformation, this long-range hopping appears as higher harmonics of the dual quasiperiodic potential, providing a controlled route for breaking the exact self-duality of the AA model, with $q$ as the control parameter, consequently modifying the localization structure for $q\neq1$. In contrast to the conventional AA model, where all eigenstates localize simultaneously at $λ_c=2$, the deformed model exhibits a fraction of delocalized states even beyond $λ_c =2$. An intermediate regime also emerges in the $q-λ$ plane where localized and extended eigenstates coexist across the spectrum. We also propose a possible experimental realization of the hierarchy produced in the $q$-deformed kinetic setting in a periodically driven AA model.

</details>


### [134] [Weakly interacting Bose-Einstein condensate with stochastic resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37110)
*Nikhil Mesquita,Manas Kulkarni,Satya N. Majumdar,Sanjib Sabhapandit*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究弱排斥玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体在随机重置协议下，内禀直接相互作用与动态涌现关联（DEC）的共存机制，并解析求解其非平衡稳态性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机重置可通过共享涨落环境在多体系统中产生强关联（DEC），但其与系统内禀直接相互作用如何共存尚不清楚，需要一个可解析处理的框架来探讨二者竞争。

Method: 在Gross-Pitaevskii框架下描述弱排斥玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体，系统从初始Thomas-Fermi态自由膨胀，并以恒定速率被随机重置回该初始态；通过解析方法求解其非平衡稳态。

Result: 重置协议将系统驱动到唯一的非平衡稳态；稳态密度在核心区保持倒抛物形式，外部呈指数衰减尾部；重置诱导凝聚体边界和粒子数出现非平凡涨落，得到了边界分布和全计数统计的精确标度形式。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究量子多体系统中内禀排斥相互作用与由共同随机环境产生的吸引性DEC之间的相互作用提供了可解析的理论框架。

Abstract: Stochastic resetting can generate strong correlations in many-body systems through a shared fluctuating environment. Here, we investigate how such dynamically emergent correlations (DEC) coexist with intrinsic (direct) interactions in a weakly repulsive Bose-Einstein condensate described within the Gross-Pitaevskii framework. The condensate undergoes free expansion from an initial Thomas-Fermi state and is stochastically reset to this initial state at a constant rate. We show that the resetting protocol drives the system into a unique nonequilibrium steady state and obtain its density profile, edge statistics, and full counting statistics analytically. The steady-state density retains an inverted-parabolic form within the core, while developing exponentially decaying tails outside it. We further show that resetting induces nontrivial fluctuations of the condensate edge and particle number, yielding exact scaling forms for the edge distribution and the full counting statistics. Our results provide a tractable setting for exploring the interplay between intrinsic repulsive interactions and attractive DEC generated by a common stochastic environment in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [135] [Derivation of the General Solution of the Black-Scholes Boundary-Value Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36447)
*ByoungSeon Choi,M. Y. Choi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文证明满足欧式看涨期权Black-Scholes偏微分方程及终端条件的函数有无穷多个，因此Black-Scholes公式并非唯一解，违反了"一价定律"，并给出这些解的严格推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Black-Scholes公式曾被视为期权定价的唯一解，并于1997年获诺贝尔经济学奖；若存在无穷多满足同一PDE与终端条件的解，则其唯一性假设不成立，并动摇了金融经济学的一价定律，故有必要严格检验解的（非）唯一性。

Method: 针对欧式看涨期权的Black-Scholes边值问题（抛物型偏微分方程+终端条件），给出满足该方程与终端条件的解族的严格数学推导，主要通过构造性方法展示无穷多解的存在性。

Result: 证明存在无穷多个函数同时满足Black-Scholes偏微分方程与欧式看涨期权的终端条件，即该边值问题的解不唯一，Black-Scholes公式并非唯一解。

Conclusion: Black-Scholes公式的唯一性假设被否证，经典定价结论与一价定律受到挑战，需重新审视解的选取标准（如边界/增长条件）以恢复唯一性。

Abstract: There are infinitely many functions that satisfy the Black-Scholes partial differential equation and the terminal condition corresponding to the European call option. This means that the Black-Scholes formula, which led to the award of the 1997 Nobel Prize in Economic Sciences, is not the unique solution, as was once assumed. Consequently, it violates the law of one price, one of the fundamental laws of economics and finance. In this article, we present a rigorous derivation of these solutions to the Black-Scholes boundary value problem.

</details>


### [136] [Exact anomalous current fluctuations in the strong-anisotropy limit of the XXZ chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37512)
*Taiki Ishiyama,Taiga Kurose,Kazuya Fujimoto,Tomohiro Sasamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究了折叠XXZ链（XXZ链的强各向异性极限）的自旋流涨落，精确求得无限温度下时间积分自旋流的矩生成函数，并证明长时极限下其归一化分布收敛到由M-Wright函数描述的非高斯分布；方法依赖于到一维t-0模型的非局域映射及其精确自旋-电荷分离，并进一步给出相邻两格点自旋之和的动力学关联函数在长时呈扩散高斯型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统中自旋流的涨落与输运性质，特别是非高斯统计与反常扩散，是理解强关联一维体系输运的核心问题；折叠XXZ链作为XXZ链强各向异性极限的可积模型，具有精确自旋-电荷分离，为研究此类涨落提供了理想平台。

Method: 采用非局域映射将折叠XXZ链等价转化为一维t-0模型，利用该模型的精确自旋-电荷分离结构，精确计算无限温度下时间积分自旋流的矩生成函数，并进行长时渐近分析；同时将该方法推广至相邻两格点自旋之和的动力学关联函数。

Result: 获得时间积分自旋流矩生成函数的精确表达式；长时渐近分析表明归一化时间积分自旋流收敛到由M-Wright函数刻画的非高斯分布；相邻两格点自旋之和的动力学关联函数在长时呈现扩散高斯剖面。

Conclusion: 折叠XXZ链中的自旋流涨落在长时由非高斯的M-Wright分布支配，突破了常规中心极限定理的高斯行为；而相邻格点自旋和的关联则表现为扩散高斯型，显示不同可观测量的输运统计存在本质差异，凸显精确自旋-电荷分离在刻画一维量子输运中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study spin-current fluctuations of the folded XXZ chain, which describes the strong-anisotropy limit of the XXZ chain. We obtain an exact expression for the moment-generating function of the time-integrated spin current at infinite temperature. A long-time asymptotic analysis establishes that the rescaled time-integrated current converges to a non-Gaussian distribution described by the M-Wright function. The derivation relies on a nonlocal map to the one-dimensional t-0 model and its exact spin-charge separation. By extending this approach, we also show that the dynamical correlation function of the spins summed over two adjacent sites exhibits a diffusive Gaussian profile at long times.

</details>


### [137] [Crossover of Scaling Behaviors of Work Cumulants in a Driven Gaussian Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36476)
*Yanbo Qiao,Ruohan Xu,H. T. Quan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文推导了受驱动O(N)高斯场论中功的特征函数(CFW)的有限温度形式，给出零温超额功各阶累积量的闭式表达，并解析刻画了APT与KZ机制的竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统非平衡功统计与临界动力学（Kibble-Zurek机制）的普适标度行为是重要问题，但APT与KZ两种机制在同一可解模型中的竞争与crossover尚缺乏系统的解析研究，且缺乏有限温度下功累积量的完整刻画。

Method: 以受驱动的O(N)高斯场论为可解模型，解析推导有限温度功特征函数(CFW)及零温超额功累积量闭式解；对有能隙protocol采用绝热微扰论(APT)分析标度；对逼近临界点、指数为p的幂律protocol，比较临界激发与正则贡献。

Result: 有能隙protocol中，若边界处一阶导数非零，则标度为τ_Q^{-2}，更光滑边界衰减更快；幂律protocol下，第n阶累积量临界贡献在d+n<2p+4时服从KZ标度τ_Q^{-p(d+n)/(p+2)}，在d+n=2p+4处出现对数修正τ_Q^{-2p}logτ_Q，在此之上标度为τ_Q^{-2p}；d为空间维度。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在可解模型中给出APT-KZ crossover的解析描述，为研究相互作用场论中两种机制的竞争提供了基准与理论基础。

Abstract: We derive the finite-temperature characteristic function of work (CFW) for a driven $O(N)$ Gaussian field theory and obtain closed-form expressions for all zero-temperature excess-work cumulants. For gapped protocols, we use adiabatic perturbation theory (APT) to derive the $τ_Q^{-2}$ scaling for protocols with a nonzero first derivative at either boundary, where $τ_Q$ is the protocol duration; smoother boundaries lead to faster decay. For power-law protocols approaching the critical point with exponent $p$, we determine the competition between critical excitations and the regular contribution. The $n$th cumulant's critical contribution follows Kibble--Zurek (KZ) scaling $τ_Q^{-p(d+n)/(p+2)}$ for $d+n<2p+4$, acquires a logarithmic correction $τ_Q^{-2p}\logτ_Q$ at $d+n=2p+4$, and scales as $τ_Q^{-2p}$ above this condition, where $d$ is the spatial dimension. This analytical study of the APT--KZ crossover in a solvable model provides a basis for studying their competition in interacting field theories.

</details>


### [138] [Quantum Statistical Thermal Engine at the BCS-BEC crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37592)
*Santiago Henríquez Lira,Felipe Isaule,Martín HvE Groves,Francisco J. Peña,Patricio Vargas,Thomás Fogarty*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双分量s波接触相互作用费米气体、工作于BCS-BEC渡越区的量子热机，其功来源于相互作用控制的统计性质；用泛函重整化群获得非微扰物态方程，构造结合Otto与Carnot特征的循环，并可在无密度变化时退化为统计型Stirling发动机，实现约38%效率及发动机/制冷机/加速器/加热器多模式可调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子热机多依赖压缩与膨胀等密度变化过程取功。本文旨在利用费米气体中由相互作用（配对关联）调控的统计性质作为新的热力学资源，探索在BCS-BEC渡越区构建不单纯依赖密度冲程的量子热机，以同时逼近Otto与Carnot效率极限并拓展器件功能。

Method: 以具有s波接触相互作用的两分量费米气体为工质，跨越BCS-BEC渡越区；采用泛函重整化群（FRG）形式，非微扰地计算包含超流-正常相变的物态方程；据此设计由等熵密度冲程、等容热化与等温相互作用扫描组成的循环。该构型集成Otto与Carnot循环特征；当密度不变时，循环退化为统计型Stirling式发动机。

Result: 该循环使系统同时饱和Otto与Carnot两个效率极限而不使净功为零。在无密度变化情形下，功完全来自相互作用，效率可达约38%。跨越渡越区出现显著不对称性，依相图中轨迹不同形成不同工作区间；同一结构可调谐为发动机、制冷机、加速器或加热器。

Conclusion: 配对关联可作为量子热机的通用热力学资源。基于相互作用调控统计性质的费米气体循环，为多模式、高效率量子热力学器件提供了新途径。

Abstract: We propose a quantum heat engine based on a two-component Fermi gas with s-wave contact interaction, operating across the BCS-BEC crossover. The work is extracted from the statistical properties of the gas, which are controlled by the interaction, rather than relying solely on compression and expansion stages. Using the functional renormalization group formalism, we obtain the equation of state in a non-perturbative form along the crossover, encompassing the superfluid-normal phase transition. The cycle combines isentropic density strokes, isochoric thermalization, and isothermal interaction sweeps. This construction makes it possible to integrate features of both Otto and Carnot cycles, in which the system simultaneously saturates both efficiency limits without the net work vanishing. In the absence of density variations, the cycle reduces to a statistical Stirling-like engine, in which the work generated arises exclusively from the interaction, achieving efficiencies up to $38\%$. A pronounced asymmetry emerges through the crossover, giving rise to distinct operating regimes depending on the trajectory followed in the phase diagram. Consequently, the same architecture can be tuned to function as an engine, refrigerator, accelerator, or heater. These findings highlight pairing correlations as a versatile thermodynamic resource for quantum heat machines.

</details>


### [139] [Static versus dynamic universality in the site-diluted kagome Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36886)
*Alexandros Vasilopoulos,Zeynep Demir Vatansever,Erol Vatansever,Gerard T. Barkema,Nikolaos G. Fytas*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文用Wolff单簇算法研究kagome格子上位点稀释Ising模型的平衡与动态临界性质：静态临界指数保持纯Ising值，动态指数z随稀释下降并趋于饱和，且kagome与方块格子在稀释区不一致，归因于二者在Ising与渗流不动点间RG流上的位置不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验位点稀释无序对Ising模型临界行为的影响，特别是区分静态与动态临界性质，并考察稀释是否引入新的动态普适类与稀释动态不动点；同时比较kagome与方块格子以判断动态普适性是否真正破坏。

Method: 采用Wolff单簇算法（一种团簇更新Monte Carlo方法）对kagome格子上的位点稀释Ising模型进行大规模数值模拟，测量平衡态临界指数ν、γ/ν及比热行为，并测定动态临界指数z；同时与方块格子的结果进行对照分析。

Result: 平衡态临界指数在所有考虑的稀释浓度p下保持纯Ising精确值ν=1、γ/ν=7/4，与无序在α=0处的边缘无关性一致；稀释后比热由对数增长过渡为双对数增长；这些静态结果在kagome与方块格子上相同。动态方面，z随稀释而减小（两格子均如此），与局域动力学下的情况相反，并在强稀释下趋于与p无关的饱和值，暗示存在稀释动态不动点；纯情形下两格子z一致，但稀释区不一致。

Conclusion: 稀释区两格子动态指数z的差异并非动态普适性的真正渐进破坏，而是因它们位于Ising与渗流不动点之间RG流的不同位置所致；静态临界性保持不变，动态行为则表现出向稀释动态不动点的饱和趋势。

Abstract: We study the site-diluted Ising model on the kagome lattice using the Wolff single-cluster algorithm, focusing on both equilibrium and dynamic critical properties. The equilibrium critical exponents $ν= 1$ and $γ/ν= 7/4$ retain their exact pure Ising values for all spin concentrations $p$ considered, consistent with the marginal irrelevance of disorder at $α= 0$, with the specific heat crossing over from logarithmic to double-logarithmic growth upon dilution. These static results are shared between the kagome and square lattices. On the dynamic side, $z$ decreases upon dilution on both lattice, opposite to what is observed under local dynamics, with evidence of saturation to a $p$-independent value at strong dilution, suggesting a diluted dynamic fixed point. While $z$ is consistent between the two lattices in the pure case, it differs in the diluted regime, an effect we attribute to the two lattices sitting at different positions along the renormalization-group flow between the Ising and percolation fixed points, rather than to a true asymptotic breakdown of dynamic universality.

</details>


### [140] [Thermodynamics of the space of trajectories governed by a combination of two additive boundary functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37102)
*V. V. Ryazanov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 将随机路径空间热力学形式体系推广到含边界与相对泛函的一般框架，并以非对称漂移+原点阶跃惩罚的OU过程为例，用Feynman-Kac与抛物柱函数数值求解本征值，给出定积分占据时间下共轭漂移流的Gallavotti-Cohen型涨落定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机路径空间热力学此前仅在特定情形下建立，缺乏对边界泛函与相对泛函的系统处理；分子布朗马达等待相、离子通道等系统涉及占据时间、积分流等非传统变量，需要统一的涨落统计描述。

Method: 系统扩展热力学变量空间，引入随机过程的边界泛函与相对泛函谱；针对一维OU过程（非对称线性漂移+原点阶跃惩罚势）建立Feynman-Kac形式，借助抛物柱函数理论，用割线法匹配对数导数构造数值方案，求解有效Hamiltonian的高精度本征值，从而得到累积量生成函数。

Result: 获得有效Hamiltonian的高精度本征值并定义累积量生成函数；对耗散半平面内固定积分占据时间下的共轭漂移流，导出Gallavotti-Cohen型修正路径空间涨落定理；阐明新变量的物理与热力学含义，并以"停留时间"与"积分流"泛函组合描述力/电刺激敏感的离子通道。

Conclusion: 该扩展框架将路径空间热力学推广至更一般的边界与相对泛函，为布朗马达等待相及离子通道等系统提供可直接数值求解的涨落统计工具和新型涨落关系。

Abstract: This paper extends the formalism of stochastic path-space thermodynamics by systematically expanding the space of thermodynamic variables with a spectrum of boundary and relative functionals of random processes. Generalizing the approaches introduced in preprints arXiv:2607.24078 and arXiv:2609.08477, we investigate the fluctuation statistics of a one-dimensional Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process with an asymmetric linear drift and a step penalty potential at the origin, which models the waiting phase of a molecular Brownian motor. Utilizing the Feynman-Kac formalism and the parabolic cylinder theory, a numerical scheme based on matching logarithmic derivatives via the secant method is constructed. A high-precision eigenvalues of the effective Hamiltonian is obtained, which define the cumulant generating function. We formulate a modified path-space fluctuation theorem of the Gallavotti-Cohen type for the conjugate drift current at a fixed integral occupation time of the system in the dissipative half-plane. The physical and thermodynamic significance of the new variables is elucidated. A combination of "residence time" and "integral current" functionals is applied to describe an ion channel sensitive to mechanical or electrical stimuli.

</details>


### [141] [Accurate ground-state entropies from population Monte Carlo: The antiferromagnetic Ising model on the Shastry-Sutherland lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37174)
*Denis Gessert,Wolfhard Janke,Martin Weigel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 利用群体退火蒙特卡罗方法高精度计算Shastry-Sutherland晶格反铁磁Ising模型的基态熵，得到每格点0.45877778(10)，与角转移矩阵重整化群结果一致，并分析了基态构型的无序特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证群体退火蒙特卡罗方法能否高精度计算基态熵，并将其应用于具有宏观简并基态的阻挫模型。

Method: 采用群体退火蒙特卡罗模拟，研究对象为Shastry-Sutherland晶格上的经典反铁磁Ising模型，对角耦合强度取最近邻相互作用的两倍，在无外磁场条件下进行计算。

Result: 测得基态熵为每格点0.45877778(10)，与角转移矩阵重整化群方法的估计吻合；通过键构型、局域能量构型和自旋关联分析基态构型，结果均与基态无序性一致，且局域能量偏离基态能级的格点团簇尺寸分布呈幂律衰减。

Conclusion: 群体退火蒙特卡罗方法可有效且高精度地计算基态熵；该模型在所有温度下无序，基态宏观简并，数值数据暗示局域能量团簇尺寸分布的幂律行为。

Abstract: We demonstrate how the population annealing Monte Carlo simulation method can be used to compute the ground-state entropy to high precision. This approach is tested for the classical antiferromagnetic Ising model on the Shastry-Sutherland lattice with diagonal couplings chosen twice as strong as the nearest-neighbor interactions. In the absence of an external magnetic field, the model is known to be disordered at all temperatures, with a macroscopically degenerate ground state. We find the ground-state entropy to be $0.458\,777\,78(10)$ per site, which is in good agreement with the estimate of a recent study using the corner transfer matrix renormalization group method. The sampled ground-state configurations are analyzed by considering the bond configurations, the local-energy configurations, and the spin-spin correlations. While all observations are consistent with the disordered nature of the ground state, the numerical data suggest a power-law decay of the size distribution of clusters of sites with local energies different from the ground-state level.

</details>


### [142] [The Spectral Cost of Detecting Nonequilibrium at Finite Temporal Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37205)
*Murad Aznagulov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 即使探测器存在最小采样间隔 $\tau_0$ 而无法取两次更近的快照，只要采样间隔由两个时钟随机化，仍能精确辨识有限马尔可夫生成元；但辨识不等于检测。对正常非平衡网络，拒绝所有隐藏平衡解释所需快照数按 $e^{2\gamma_{\mathrm{irr}}\tau_0}$ 增长，$\gamma_{\mathrm{irr}}$ 为旋转模最慢阻尼率；该速率是自适应策略的极限，且对单条相依轨迹的检验可达此界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于突破"探测器响应速度有限、最小采样间隔 $\tau_0$ 约束"下的马尔可夫生成元辨识问题，并区分"辨识（identification）"与"检测（detection）"两个概念：现有工作多关注能否辨识生成元，而本文追问在正常非平衡网络中，拒绝所有隐平衡解释所需的样本复杂度及其极限。

Method: 方法上，利用两个时钟对采样间隔进行随机化，从而绕过固定最小间隔 $\tau_0$ 的限制，实现有限马尔可夫生成元的精确辨识；随后刻画正常非平衡网络中的旋转模，以 $\gamma_{\mathrm{irr}}$ 度量最慢阻尼；进一步分析自适应调度与基于单条相依轨迹的检验方案，导出快照数下界与可达性。

Result: 结果为：随机化间隔下，受 $\tau_0$ 限制的探测器仍可精确辨识有限马尔可夫生成元；对于正常非平衡网络，拒绝所有隐藏平衡解释所需快照数随 $e^{2\gamma_{\mathrm{irr}}\tau_0}$ 增长；不存在自适应调度能优于该速率，而单条相依轨迹上的检验可达到该速率。

Conclusion: 结论是：辨识能力受采样间隔限制但不被其破坏，检测隐平衡解释的成本由旋转模最慢阻尼 $\gamma_{\mathrm{irr}}$ 决定，$e^{2\gamma_{\mathrm{irr}}\tau_0}$ 为该问题的根本极限，且可通过单轨迹检验实现，说明检测与辨识在资源复杂度上具有本质区别。

Abstract: A detector that cannot take two snapshots closer than a time $τ_0$ still identifies a fully observed finite Markov generator exactly, provided its gaps are randomized by two clocks. Identification is not detection. For a normal nonequilibrium network, the number of snapshots needed to reject every hidden equilibrium explanation grows as $e^{2γ_{\mathrm{irr}}τ_0}$, where $γ_{\mathrm{irr}}$ is the slowest damping of rotating modes. No adaptive schedule beats this rate, and a test on one dependent trajectory attains it.

</details>


### [143] [Scale invariance, fractal dynamics, and critical exponents at the phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37275)
*Henrique Alves de Lima*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出临界点平衡动力学有效演化于分形子空间而非完整欧氏空间，从Fisher序参量关联函数出发，用分数阶微积分将反常临界指数η几何化为关联分形维数d_R，并推导其与Riesz分数阶导数的显式关系，进而检验Rushbrooke标度律及非整数维与无序体系的临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界现象中反常临界指数η通常缺乏直观的几何解释，作者希望为η和标度律提供一个基于分形几何的统一理解框架。

Method: 以Fisher的序参量关联函数表述为起点，引入与有效子空间关联的关联分形维数来几何诠释η；借助分数阶微积分（特别是Riesz分数阶导数）推导关联函数形式，使其在上临界维以下恢复临界行为，并建立η与分形维数d_R的显式关系；同时考察Rushbrooke标度关系，并在非整数维及由参数σ控制的无序体系中检验该几何解释的适用范围。

Result: 得到了在上临界维以下正确重现临界行为的关联函数表达式，并建立η与Riesz分数阶导数所联系的分形维数d_R之间的显式关系；在非整数维和无序体系中确定了该几何解释保持有效的参数范围。

Conclusion: 标度律、临界指数、关联与分形几何在此框架下相互联系，临界点的反常行为可理解为动力学被约束在有效分形子空间上的结果。

Abstract: We propose that, at criticality, equilibrium dynamics effectively evolves on a fractal subspace rather than throughout the full Euclidean space. Starting from Fisher's formulation of the order-parameter correlation function, we interpret the anomalous critical exponent $η$ geometrically through a correlation fractal dimension associated with this effective subspace. Using fractional-calculus tools, we derive a form of the correlation function that recovers critical behavior below the upper critical dimension and obtain an explicit relation between $η$ and a fractal dimension $d_R$ linked to the Riesz fractional derivative. We also examine the Rushbrooke scaling relation and investigate critical behavior in non-integer-dimensional and disordered systems. In the disordered case, controlled by a parameter $σ$, we test the range over which the proposed geometric interpretation remains valid. The results connect scaling laws, critical exponents, correlations, and fractal geometry, suggesting that the anomalous behavior observed at criticality can be understood as a consequence of dynamics constrained to an effective fractal subspace.

</details>


### [144] [Anomalous transport in periodic systems driven by active fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37289)
*Karol Białas*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究周期势场中由活性涨落（非高斯白泊松散粒噪声）驱动的布朗粒子输运，系统揭示巨幅输运增强、绝对负迁移率和棘轮效应三大反常输运机制，并结合蒙特卡洛模拟与解析建模给出定量解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性涨落打破细致平衡并违反涨落-耗散定理，可刻画生物与合成微系统中的自驱动机制及活性浴相互作用，但驱动输运的反常机制尚需系统澄清。

Method: 采用具有非高斯幅度统计的白泊松散粒噪声模型，结合蒙特卡洛模拟与解析建模；提出唯象跳跃-弛豫模型，将位移分解为瞬时随机跳跃与向势阱极小值的确定性弛豫，并推广至含惯性动力学；建立一维纯过阻尼的极简绝对负迁移率模型。

Result: 双向偏正态幅度分布使定向速度较自由粒子提升数个数量级；有限质量m可作为动态控制参量，依噪声方差增强、削弱、诱导或完全抑制输运增强；在纯过阻尼一维系统中，间断散粒噪声无需惯性、周期势非线性或显式周期驱动即可产生逆偏置输运；在另一平均随机力标度下，幅度分布不对称可在零偏置极限通过倾斜棘轮效应产生净定向输运。

Conclusion: 非高斯活性涨落可单独驱动多种反常输运现象，跳跃-弛豫框架与极简模型为定量描述和调控活性物质输运提供了统一的理论工具。

Abstract: This dissertation investigates the transport of Brownian particles driven by active fluctuations in periodic potential landscapes. Unlike thermal noise, active fluctuations break detailed balance and violate the fluctuation-dissipation theorem. They model self-propulsion mechanisms and active-bath interactions in biological and synthetic micro-systems. By focusing on white Poisson shot noise with non-Gaussian amplitude statistics, this work systematically uncovers the mechanisms governing three major transport anomalies: giant transport enhancement, absolute negative mobility, and the ratchet effect.
  Combining Monte Carlo simulations with analytical modeling, this work demonstrates that active fluctuations adhering to a bidirectional skew-normal amplitude distribution enhance directed velocity compared to the free-particle by several orders of magnitude. This giant transport enhancement is fully quantified via a phenomenological jump-relaxation model that decouples particle displacement into instantaneous stochastic jumps and deterministic relaxation towards potential minimum. Extending this model to inertial dynamics reveals that finite particle mass m acts as a dynamic control parameter capable of strengthening, weakening, constructively inducing, or completely suppressing transport enhancement depending on noise variance.
  Furthermore, an ultra-minimal model for absolute negative mobility is established in a one-dimensional, purely overdamped system, showing that discontinuous shot noise drives transport opposite to an applied bias without requiring particle inertia, nonlinearity of spatially periodic potential, or explicit time-dependent periodic driving. Conversely, under an alternative scaling of the average stochastic force, an asymmetric distribution of fluctuation amplitudes generates net directed transport through a tilting ratchet effect in the zero-bias limit.

</details>


### [145] [Phase separation, morphology, and metastability in the three-dimensional active Potts model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37436)
*Aditya Kumar Dutta,Raja Paul*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究三维立方晶格上的活性Potts模型（取向态数为2、4、6），给出稳态相图并揭示推进速度诱导的致密相形貌转变与极性液的亚稳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将此前二维活性Potts模型的研究拓展到三维，考察维度提升与取向态数目增加对相行为、致密相形貌及极性液稳定性的影响。

Method: 在立方晶格上构建三维活性Potts模型（q=2,4,6），结合微观模拟与粗粒化流体力学方程，分析稳态相图、致密相形貌演化，并考察人工引入液滴后极性液的稳定性。

Result: 稳态相图包含低密度气态、高密度极性液态及液-气共存相，由密度、温度和推进速度调控，并保留二维APM的主要特征。弱推进时致密相形成垂直于运动方向的板状结构；随推进速度增加，2态模型保持该形貌，4态与6态致密相发生重取向并沿纵向传播，6态进一步形成圆柱形貌。高推进速度下，反向传播液滴仅能部分改变初始极性态，横向液滴则能完全改变之，表明三维APM中极性液具有亚稳性。

Conclusion: 三维活性Potts模型保留了二维模型的核心相行为，但取向态数目显著影响致密相形貌；极性液在三维中为亚稳态，其稳定性强烈依赖于液滴取向与推进速度。

Abstract: We study the three-dimensional active Potts model (APM) on a cubic lattice with 2, 4, and 6 orientational states. The steady-state phase diagrams exhibit low-density gaseous, high-density polar liquid, and liquid--gas coexistence phases, with the phase behavior controlled by density, temperature, and propulsion speed and retaining the main features of the previously studied two-dimensional APM. At weak propulsion, the dense phase forms a slab perpendicular to its direction of motion. With increasing propulsion speed, this morphology persists for the 2-state model, whereas the dense phases for 4 and 6 states reorient and propagate along their longitudinal direction. For the 6-state model, the reoriented dense phase further develops a cylindrical morphology. We also examine the stability of polar liquids against artificially introduced droplets using microscopic simulations and coarse-grained hydrodynamic equations. At high propulsion speeds, counter-propagating droplets can partially modify the initial polar state, while transverse droplets can completely alter it, demonstrating the metastable nature of polar liquids in the three-dimensional active Potts model.

</details>


### [146] [Blind directions of linear response and the limits of finite-perturbation bounds on efficiency fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37940)
*Badr Farih*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将Barankin多点界应用于倾斜路径测度，构造了一个可计算工具，用于刻画有限扰动下非线性观测量（如㶲效率η）的方差可认证程度，在传统Cramér-Rao界为零的方向上仍给出正的方差下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学不确定关系只能约束时间反对称流的涨落，而随机效率η=W/(W+T₀S)在路径反演下为偶数、非反对称量，且传统比值-W/Q_h不存在矩。因此需要一种能处理非线性观测量、且在线性响应失效（Cramér-Rao界为零）时仍能认证方差的方法。

Method: 以㶲效率η（其矩存在）为对象，在速率与位点扰动下比较Cramér-Rao界与Barankin多点界；将Barankin界应用于倾斜路径测度，利用倾斜马尔可夫跳过程路径测度的Gram矩阵——一个Feynman-Kac矩阵指数，其势为跳强度的Hellinger被积函数——并以扰动的χ²散度作为代价；对Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程给出精确可解构造。

Result: 对任意流以及任何关于跃迁计数与停留时间线性的观测量，多参数Cramér-Rao界已等于方差；而对η则不然：Cramér-Rao界在一个扰动方向超平面上消失，而Barankin多点界在该处仍为正。在三态马达模型中，当所有线性响应界为零时，该界恢复η方差的75%；整体上该界达到最佳线性响应界的1.25–1.57倍，且增益在最多十六个状态的网络上持续存在。对OU过程，最优倾斜按T^{-1/2}标度，三个测试点几乎达到上确界。此外证明两个极限：增益是短窗口效应，随计数趋于高斯而消失；该界无法转为推断，粗测只能从下方界定χ²。

Conclusion: 该构造是面向已知模型的计算工具，而非不确定关系：它能在有限扰动、短时间窗口下为非线性效率观测量提供非平凡的方差下界，且不被耗散界所控制，但不能用于推断，增益最终随高斯极限消失。

Abstract: Thermodynamic uncertainty relations bound the fluctuations of time-antisymmetric currents. Stochastic efficiency is not one: a ratio of two odd quantities, it is even under path reversal, and for the conventional ratio $-W/Q_h$ no moment exists. We work with the exergetic ratio $η=W/(W+T_0S)$, whose moments exist, and ask how much of its variance a finite perturbation of the dynamics can certify when linear response cannot. The question is specific to nonlinear observables: for every current, and every observable linear in transition counts and residence times, the multiparameter Cramér-Rao bound over rate and site perturbations already equals the variance. For $η$ it does not. The Cramér-Rao bound vanishes on a hyperplane of perturbation directions, while the Barankin multi-point bound, applied to tilted path measures, stays positive there and recovers 75% of the variance of $η$ in a three-state motor model where every linear-response bound is zero. The Gram matrix of tilted Markov-jump path measures is a Feynman-Kac matrix exponential whose potential is the Hellinger integrand of the jump intensities, and the cost of a perturbation is its $χ^2$ divergence, which no dissipation bound controls. The bound captures 1.25-1.57 times the best linear-response bound, and the gain persists on networks of up to sixteen states. For an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process the construction is exactly solvable: the optimal tilt scales as $T^{-1/2}$ and three test points nearly reach the supremum. Two limits are established. The gain is a short-window effect that disappears as the counts become Gaussian, and the bound cannot be turned into inference: coarse measurements bound $χ^2$ only from below. The construction is a computational instrument for a model in hand, not an uncertainty relation.

</details>


### [147] [The Principle of Minimum Justified Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38124)
*John H Van Drie*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文（上篇）指出香农熵、Fisher信息与量子动能均可视为相关性度量，并证明在"相关性破坏映射"下三者一致地减小，进而提出"最小正当相关性原理"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为熵、Fisher信息与量子动能这些看似不同的物理/信息量找到统一的概念基础，即以"相关性"为共同本质，并借此为选择分布或波函数提供一个变分判据。

Method: 以基本的相关性破坏映射为出发点：将联合概率密度 ρ(x,y) 替换为边际乘积 ρ_x(x)ρ_y(y)。离散情形下香农熵在此映射下非减；连续情形下构造坐标不变的相关性度量 I[ρ]=h[ρ_x]+h[ρ_y]−h[ρ]。进一步引入相对 Fisher 信息 J(ρ‖ρ_xρ_y)，并针对归一化实量子波函数推导其与平均动能减少量的精确比例关系。

Result: 在连续情形下得到坐标不变的相关性度量 I[ρ]=h[ρ_x]+h[ρ_y]−h[ρ]；相对 Fisher 信息 J(ρ‖ρ_xρ_y) 在该映射下行为类似；并证明 ⟨T⟩_ψ−⟨T⟩_Φ=(ℏ²/8m)J(ρ‖ρ_xρ_y)，其中 Φ=√(ρ_xρ_y)，即动能的减少正比于相对 Fisher 信息。

Conclusion: 熵、Fisher 信息与量子动能可在相关性框架下统一理解，支持 Jaynes 意义上的"最小正当相关性原理"：在给定物理约束及满足约束的分布或振幅集合中，应选取相关性最小者。本文为上篇，仅建立恒等式并陈述该原理；下篇将讨论多重解、时间依赖、自旋作用、通向薛定谔方程的路径及实验检验设想。

Abstract: It is shown that Shannon entropy, Fisher information, and quantum-mechanical kinetic energy may all be viewed as measures of correlation. We begin with a fundamental correlation-destroying map: a joint probability density $ρ(x,y)$ is replaced by $ρ_x(x)ρ_y(y)$, the product of its marginal distributions. In the discrete case, Shannon's entropy is nondecreasing under this map. For continuous distributions, this fundamental map leads to a well-behaved, coordinate-invariant correlation measure, analogous to the discrete Shannon entropy: \[ I[ρ] = h[ρ_x] + h[ρ_y] - h[ρ]. \] In the continuous case, however, another measure appears: Fisher information. The relative Fisher information $J(ρ\|ρ_xρ_y)$ behaves similarly under the fundamental map. For a normalized real quantum wavefunction, this decrease is exactly proportional to the decrease in mean kinetic energy, \[ \langle T\rangle_ψ- \langle T\rangle_Φ= \frac{\hbar^2}{8m} J(ρ\|ρ_xρ_y), \qquad Φ= \sqrt{ρ_x ρ_y}. \] In Jaynes's language, the result supports a principle of minimum justified correlation: given physical constraints and a set of possible distributions or related amplitudes satisfying those constraints, select from that set those with the least correlation.
  This is Part I of a two-part paper. Here we develop the entropy, Fisher-information, and kinetic-energy identities above, and state the principle they support. Part II addresses questions which Part I raises but leaves unanswered: multiple solutions, time dependence, the role of spin, a route to the Schrödinger equation itself, and a proposed experimental test. AI has been used.

</details>


### [148] [Classification of topological phases of matter in stochastic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38171)
*Dexin Li,Evelyn Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对随机（马尔可夫）系统中的拓扑相缺乏一致分类的问题，提出新的同伦方法并重新定义点隙，建立了适用于马尔可夫约束的拓扑分类框架，发现仅"无对称性"和"伪厄米性"两类对称类保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑相在量子系统及类似平台中已有成熟的按对称性与维度分类的理论，且随机系统中也发现了拓扑相并被推测为生化过程的机制。然而，由于马尔可夫约束改变了矩阵空间，先前分类方法无法直接适用，同时随机系统还呈现诸如非厄米性对非平凡拓扑相的必要性等新特性，因此亟需一套与马尔可夫约束一致的分类，以推广到新场景。

Method: 作者引入新的方法，包括一种新的同伦（homotopy）方法，并重新定义点隙（point gap），以适配马尔可夫约束下被改变的矩阵空间，进而对随机系统中的拓扑相进行系统分类。

Result: 在马尔可夫约束下，仅有两种对称类保持稳健：无对称性（no symmetry）与伪厄米性（pseudo-Hermiticity）。作者确定了这两类对称类中存在拓扑非平凡相的维度及其群结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为随机系统中的拓扑相建立了严格的分类框架，可用于预测活性和生命物质（active and living matter）中的稳健行为。

Abstract: Topological phases of matter support edge states protected from noise and perturbations, and the classification of which symmetry and dimension support such phases was developed for quantum systems and analogous platforms. While topological phases have also been discovered in stochastic systems and posited as mechanisms for biochemical processes, a classification consistent with their Markovian constraints remains lacking, impeding generalization to new scenarios. These constraints alter the matrix space preventing the application of previous classification methods, while hosting new properties such as the necessity of non-Hermiticity for non-trivial topological phases. We introduce new methods including a new homotopy approach and redefinition of the point gap and find that only two symmetry classes remain robust: no symmetry and pseudo-Hermiticity. In these symmetry classes, we identify the dimensions with topologically non-trivial phases and their group structure, creating a rigorous framework for predicting robust behavior in active and living matter.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Multicomponent anyons in one-dimensional optical lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36554)
*Sagarika Basak,Xi-Wen Guan,Han Pu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究一维光晶格中多组分相互作用任意子的基态与动力学性质，发现分数交换统计与多组分效应共同重构自旋和电荷关联，并可调控淬火动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多组分（自旋）效应与分数交换统计相结合如何影响一维光晶格中任意子体系的基态关联与动力学行为，拓展对统计驱动的相变的理解。

Method: 采用任意子—Hubbard模型（Anyon-Hubbard model），以相互作用强度和任意子统计参数为变量，计算自旋与电荷关联、动量分布、自旋结构因子，并开展淬火动力学分析。

Result: 分数交换统计使自旋费米子动量分布中原本对称的壳层结构及奇异峰发生重构，出现不对称性、新谱峰和展宽奇异特征；作为极限情形的赝玻色子不再呈现占主导的零动量峰，而表现出带有限动量峰的准费米子壳层结构，其位置由格点占据数和相互作用强度调控；自旋结构因子显示反铁磁序，关联强度可由任意子交换统计调节；淬火动力学中统计相位参数可切换偶极振荡的欠阻尼/过阻尼弛豫并控制云团膨胀。

Conclusion: 统计相位参数可作为有效调控旋钮，实现统计驱动的基态与动力学相变，为自旋任意子体系研究提供新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the ground-state and dynamical properties of multicomponent interacting anyons confined in a one-dimensional (1D) optical lattice. Adopting the Anyon--Hubbard model, we explore spin and charge correlations as functions of interaction strength and the anyonic statistical parameter. Our results demonstrate that multicomponent effects combined with fractional exchange statistics substantially reshape both charge and spin correlations. For fractional exchange statistics, the canonical symmetric shell structure with prominent singularities in the spinor fermionic momentum distribution undergoes notable structural reconstruction, exhibiting emergent asymmetry, new spectral peaks, and broadened singular features. Unlike the Tonks--Girardeau bosonic gas, pseudobosons, representing a limiting case of anyons, cease to display a dominant zero-momentum peak. Instead, a quasi-fermionic shell structure emerges with finite-momentum peaks, whose locations are modulated by lattice site occupancy and interaction strength. The structure factor of spin correlations uncovers antiferromagnetic ordering in spinor anyon systems, with correlation magnitudes tunable by anyonic exchange statistics. Furthermore, quench dynamics analysis reveals the statistical phase parameter as an effective tuning knob. It enables the switching of dipole oscillations between underdamped and overdamped relaxation regimes and governs overall cloud expansion dynamics. Our findings pave the way for exploring statistics-driven ground-state and dynamical phase transitions in spinor anyon systems.

</details>


### [150] [Generalized Effective Spin-Chain formalism for multicomponent anyons in one-dimensional optical lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36555)
*Sagarika Basak,Xi-Wen Guan,Han Pu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出广义有效自旋链(GESC)形式化框架，用于描述一维光晶格中强相互作用多组分任意子，实现自旋-电荷分离并揭示分数交换统计在静态与动力学中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强相互作用多组分任意子的统计效应难以直接刻画，需建立能连接微观虚交换过程与宏观动力学的有效理论框架。

Method: 将粒子运动映射为无自旋费米子、自旋态映射为有序链，构建GESC形式化；在强相互作用区推导有效电荷与自旋哈密顿量。

Result: 领头阶电荷哈密顿量不依赖统计相位，统计相位经由双占据态虚隧穿进入自旋交换系数；非平衡下连续自旋交换累积方向依赖相位，驱动输运从色散向局域化跨界，并在中间统计下产生非对称传播。

Conclusion: GESC是高效通用框架，将宏观动力学与微观虚交换过程相连，提供自旋-电荷分离视角以理解分数交换统计在强相互作用下的存活与表现。

Abstract: We develop a generalized effective spin-chain (GESC) formalism for strongly interacting multicomponent anyons in a one-dimensional (1D) optical lattice. By mapping particle motion onto spinless fermions and spin states onto an ordered chain, this framework provides a spin--charge-separated perspective on the physical effects of fractional exchange statistics. In the strong-interaction regime, the leading-order charge Hamiltonian becomes independent of the statistical phase; instead, virtual tunneling through doubly occupied states directly imprints this phase on the spin-exchange coefficients. The GESC formalism captures ground-state properties of the full Anyon--Hubbard model reported in S.~Basak, X.-W.~Guan, and H.~Pu, unpublished manuscript (2026), and reveals that their statistical dependence is predominantly encoded in the explicit anyonic structure of the observables rather than the underlying spin ground state. This formalism uncovers that, out of equilibrium, successive spin exchanges accumulate direction-dependent phases; the resulting interference drives crossover from dispersive to localized impurity transport and generates inversion-asymmetric propagation at intermediate statistics. Suppressed expansion persists for identical and distinguishable impurities, with differences in propagation governed by the interplay between impurity identity and interaction anisotropy. Ultimately, GESC offers a versatile, computationally efficient theoretical framework that connects macroscopic dynamics to microscopic virtual exchange processes, conferring a unique spin--charge-separated vantage for resolving how fractional exchange statistics survives strong interactions and manifests distinctly across static and dynamical regimes.

</details>


### [151] [Topological Pseudo-Goldstone Modes from Weakly Broken Dipole Conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36994)
*Yan-Guang Yue,Jie Lou,Yan Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在有序相中弱破缺连续对称性可将Goldstone模式转化为拓扑集体能带；作者在中性偶极子凝聚体中建立该机制，弱实味混合选取手性凝聚体，陀螺耦合作用于缠绕极化织构产生含拓扑最低带的赝Goldstone扇区，恢复偶极守恒后两模式在零频相遇、各自Chern数失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续对称性破缺产生的Goldstone模式通常是无能隙的平庸激发，缺乏拓扑性质。作者希望探索一种普适机制：通过弱破缺连续对称性，把Goldstone模式转化为具有拓扑性质的集体能带，并在可调控的中性偶极子凝聚体平台中实现。

Method: 以中性偶极子凝聚体为平台，其守恒母对称性支持两个独立的Goldstone相位模式；引入弱实味混合钉扎相位并选取手性凝聚体；陀螺耦合作用于缠绕极化织构，产生含拓扑最低带的赝Goldstone扇区；利用母偶极荷定义的张量流及其圆响应，在基准区间重建低能嵌入局域Berry曲率；并给出解析稳定性边界与大占据数极限。

Result: 弱实味混合使两个独立Goldstone相位模式被钉扎并选出手性凝聚体；陀螺耦合生成含拓扑最低带的赝Goldstone扇区；恢复偶极守恒时两模式在零频相遇，各自能带的Chern数不再定义；张量流的圆响应可用于模型辅助重建低能嵌入局域Berry曲率；获得解析稳定性边界与大占据数极限。

Conclusion: 弱破缺连续对称性是产生拓扑集体能带的可行机制，并在中性偶极子凝聚体中得到受控实现；Chern数的失效与张量流圆响应的重建能力为该机制的实验探测与表征提供途径。

Abstract: Weakly breaking a continuous symmetry in an ordered phase can convert its Goldstone modes into topological collective bands. We establish this mechanism in a neutral dipole condensate whose conserving parent supports two independent Goldstone phase modes. Weak real flavor mixing pins the phases and selects a chiral condensate. The resulting gyroscopic coupling acts on their winding polarization texture, producing a pseudo-Goldstone sector containing a topological lowest band. Restoring dipole conservation forces the two modes to meet at zero frequency, where their separate band Chern numbers cease to be defined. The same parent dipole charges define a tensor current whose circular response permits model-assisted reconstruction of low-energy embedded local Berry curvature in the benchmark regime. Analytical stability bounds and a large-occupation limit provide a controlled realization of this mechanism.

</details>


### [152] [Finite-momentum trimers and Cooper quartets in a one-dimensional Fermi gas with coexistent $s$- and $p$-wave interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37032)
*Yixin Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在一维双组分费米气体中，当s波与p波相互作用共存且按相对动量正规化（保持伽利略不变性）时，有限动量三体（trimer）和库珀四体（quartet）普遍存在：泡利阻塞使trimer色散偏离自由质心运动，其能量可在有限总动量处最低；aabb四体始终低于所有三体分支，并在弱种间吸引下由两个奇波库珀对束缚成四体，其束缚能可远大于共存trimer，且费米海会增强弱耦合四体束缚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于：以往对少体束缚态（三体、四体）的讨论常以最强束缚对或最大相互作用能作为判据，但在同时存在s波与p波相互作用、且体系处于费米海中的多体环境下，这一判据是否成立并不清楚。作者希望厘清有限动量三体与库珀四体的形成条件、动量选择性、以及费米海（泡利阻塞与密度）对多体束缚的影响。

Method: 采用一维双组分费米气体模型，同时包含s波与p波相互作用，并对每一对的相对动量进行正规化以保持伽利略不变性。通过求解有限总动量下的三体（abb）与四体（aabb）束缚态问题，确定其色散关系与最低能量；用变分束缚方法在弱种间吸引极限下证明两个奇波库珀对可束缚为四体；并系统扫描耦合强度、散射长度与密度，考察各分支（二体、三体、四体）的相对稳定性与解离通道。

Result: 1）仅由泡利阻塞即可使trimer色散偏离自由质心运动，其能量在有限总动量处最低；允许该运动后，abb区域（相对于对与trimer）扩大，并消除了奇波对最低的区域。2）在每个采样耦合下，aabb四体都低于上述所有分支；在始于两个二体对通道交叉的条带内，其最低解离通道是一个运动中的abb trimer加一个原子。3）弱种间吸引时，变分束缚给出两个奇波库珀对结合成四体，且随吸引减弱，其束缚能比共存trimer高出数量级。4）在较强种间吸引时，若关闭奇波相互作用，四体相对于两个异种二体的额外束缚消失，尽管偶波相互作用主导相互作用能。5）相对于真空情形，费米海增强弱耦合四体束缚；在固定散射长度下，四体束缚随密度增大，而共存trimer显著变浅。

Conclusion: 结论是：判断四体是否束缚不能仅依据最强束缚对或主导相互作用能，必须同时考虑有限动量、奇波通道、泡利阻塞与费米海密度效应等多体机制。这些结果揭示了在一维s+p波费米气体中，三体与四体束缚具有动量选择性和密度依赖的非平庸结构。

Abstract: We study finite-momentum trimers and Cooper quartets in a one-dimensional two-component Fermi gas with coexistent $s$- and $p$-wave interactions, regularized in the relative momentum of each pair so that they remain Galilean invariant. Pauli blocking alone then makes the trimer dispersion deviate from free center-of-mass motion, so the trimer energy can be lowest at finite total momentum. Allowing this motion expands the $abb$ region among pairs and trimers and removes the region where an odd-wave pair is lowest. The $aabb$ quartet lies below all these branches at every sampled coupling, and in a strip starting on the crossing of the two dimer-pair channels its lowest breakup channel is a moving $abb$ trimer and an atom. At weak interspecies attraction, a variational bound shows that two odd-wave Cooper pairs bind into a quartet, whose binding exceeds that of the coexisting trimer by orders of magnitude as the attraction weakens. At stronger interspecies attraction, the quartet binding relative to two unlike-species dimers disappears when the odd-wave interaction is switched off, although the even-wave interaction dominates the interaction energy. Relative to the in-vacuum case, the Fermi sea enhances the weak-coupling quartet binding, and at fixed scattering lengths the quartet binding grows with density while the coexisting trimer becomes much shallower. These results show that neither the most strongly bound pair nor the dominant interaction energy alone determines whether the quartet binds.

</details>


### [153] [Quantum Simulation of Detuning-Controlled Proximity-Induced Localization and Reentrant Delocalization in a Quasiperiodic Ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37059)
*Mrinal Kanti Giri,Shih Tai-Ming,Pinaki Sengupta,Pochung Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 通过在准周期梯形晶格中仅对一条腿施加交错失谐，可邻近诱导另一条腿的局域化，并在失谐继续增大时出现再入离域，形成离域-局域-离域序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在耦合准周期系统中通过可控方式诱导和逆转局域化，尤其是无需直接修改目标链的准周期势。

Method: 在失谐准周期梯形模型中，对一条腿施加交错失谐，通过腿间杂化影响另一条腿；使用静态局域化诊断量刻画相区，并在IBM量子硬件上测量可实验观测的动力学特征。

Result: 弱准周期区域中原本扩展的Aubry-André链，在相邻腿失谐作用下通过腿间杂化被诱导局域化；继续增大失谐后纯链重新进入离域态。实验观测到邻近局域区波函数扩散被强烈抑制，而更大失谐时扩散能力再入恢复。

Conclusion: 腿选择性失谐可作为可编程控制旋钮，用于在耦合准周期系统中诱导和逆转局域化。

Abstract: We demonstrate proximity-induced localization and re-entrant delocalization in a detuned quasiperiodic ladder. In the weak-quasiperiodic regime, where uncoupled Aubry-André chains are extended, a staggered detuning applied to only one leg induces localization in the neighboring leg through inter-leg hybridization. With further increasing detuning, the pure chain re-enters a delocalized regime, producing a delocalized-localized-delocalized sequence without directly modifying its quasiperiodic potential. We identify these regimes using static localization diagnostics and demonstrate experimentally accessible dynamical signatures on IBM Quantum hardware: strongly suppressed wavefunction spreading in the proximity-localized regime and its re-entrant recovery at larger detuning. These results establish leg-selective detuning as a programmable control knob for inducing and reversing localization in coupled quasiperiodic systems.

</details>


### [154] [Second-order coherence properties of ultrafast polariton dynamics in plasmonic lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37142)
*Evgeny A. Mamonov,Lukas Freter,Sioneh Eyvazi,Elliot W. Lloyd,Päivi Törmä*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作测量了等离激元晶格中极化激元发射的二阶相干性，Γ点与非Γ点辐射均显示极高相干度（g^(2)(0)-1<10^-4），并揭示超快（>1 THz）动力学，将过程解释为超快热化的BEC或超辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合体系中极化激元激光与BEC的一阶相干性研究结果一致，但二阶相干性因体系不同而差异显著，甚至阈值以上仍出现超泊松涨落（偏离原子BEC的g^(2)=1），亟需深入理解光基凝聚体的发射统计与动力学，尤其是非平衡区。

Method: 在等离激元晶格中测量极化激元发射的二阶相干函数，并分别针对Γ点（基态）与非Γ点（高能尾部）辐射进行分析，同时表征其动力学时间尺度。

Result: 在Γ点与非Γ点辐射均观测到高度相干，g^(2)(τ=0)-1<10^-4；并证实动力学为超快过程（>1 THz）。

Conclusion: 该过程可解释为具有亚50 fs超快热化的BEC，作者同时提出另一种可能解释：在具有振动自由度的发射体体系中的超辐射。

Abstract: The first-order coherence properties of strongly coupled systems supporting polariton lasing and Bose-Einstein condensation (BEC) have been thoroughly studied and show consistent results. In contrast, second-order coherence properties have been found to vary for different systems, and the second-order coherence function can deviate from the value of one, typical for atomic BEC, revealing super-Poissonian fluctuations even above the BEC/polariton lasing threshold. This calls for a deeper understanding of the emission statistics and dynamics in light-based condensates, especially in the non-equilibrium regime. Here we demonstrate a coherent state of polariton emission from a plasmonic lattice measured as the second-order coherence function value separately for $\mathrmΓ$- and non-$\mathrmΓ$-point radiation (ground state and high-energy tail). We observe a high degree of coherence with the value $g^{(2)}(τ=0)-1<10^{-4}$. We also demonstrate the ultrafast ($>$1 THz) nature of the dynamics. The process can be interpreted as a BEC with ultrafast, sub-50-fs thermalization, but we also suggest an alternative explanation as superradiance in a system where the emitters have vibrational degrees of freedom.

</details>
