### [155] [Advancing Fuzzy-Sphere CFTs: $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-Rotation-Resolving Exact Diagonalization, 3D Ising Heavy Primaries, and Quasi-Hole-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35960)
*Zheng Zhou,Yin-Chen He*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过对称性与希尔伯特空间结构改进模糊球正则化框架，实现精确对角化并显著降低计算成本，在三维Ising CFT中发现约100个初级算符，并揭示与拓扑序交织的CFT研究策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊球正则化已成为研究三维共形场论（CFT）的有力框架，但现有方法在计算成本和对称性利用上存在局限；同时此前模糊球与bootstrap结果在宇称奇标量初级算符上存在矛盾，亟需更高效、更精确的处理手段。

Method: 利用对称性和希尔伯特空间结构，开发了精确对角化算法，完整解析SO(3)旋转对称性；并构造由准空穴态（如Laughlin态）张成的显著缩减的希尔伯特空间来研究涉及分数量子霍尔态的临界点。

Result: 计算成本降低数百倍；在三维Ising CFT中识别出约100个初级算符，包括大量此前未知的、标度维数高达Δ≈16.5的重标量初级算符；解决了宇称奇标量初级算符上模糊球与bootstrap的矛盾；识别出与φ^4拉格朗日描述中φ^n算符相关的标量初级算符分支，始于identity、σ、ε、ε'、σ'，其波函数呈现与球面保面积微分同胚不变态密切相关的半经典结构。

Conclusion: 该工作大幅推进了模糊球正则化框架，为系统研究三维CFT谱结构提供了高效工具，并提出了探索与拓扑有序相交织的CFT的一般性策略。

Abstract: Fuzzy-sphere regularization has recently emerged as a powerful framework for studying three-dimensional conformal field theories (CFTs). In this work, we exploit symmetry and Hilbert-space structures to substantially advance this framework. We develop an exact-diagonalization algorithm that resolves the full $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ rotational symmetry, reducing computational costs by factors of several hundred. For the three-dimensional Ising CFT, we identify around 100 primary operators, including a large number of previously unknown heavy scalar primaries up to scaling dimension $Δ\approx16.5$. We also resolve the tension between previous fuzzy-sphere and bootstrap results regarding the parity-odd scalar primary. Furthermore, we identify a branch of scalar primaries associated with $φ^n$ operators in the $φ^4$ Lagrangian description, beginning with the identity, $σ$, $ε$, $ε'$, and $σ'$. Their wave functions exhibit a semi-classical structure closely related to states invariant under area-preserving diffeomorphisms of the sphere. Finally, we show that critical points involving fractional quantum Hall states can be studied within a substantially reduced Hilbert space spanned by quasi-hole states of the corresponding model quantum Hall wave functions like the Laughlin state. This suggests a general strategy for exploring CFTs intertwined with topologically ordered phases.

</details>


### [156] [Spectroscopy of phonon-coupled integer and fractional Chern insulators: emergence of polarons and chirality deficit of graviton mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36027)
*Min Long,Yuzhu Wang,Zi Yang Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用局域基优化的密度矩阵重整化群方法，研究平带Haldane模型与动力学Holstein声子在整数及分数填充下的耦合，揭示声子主要通过缀饰电中性集体模式（激子与磁旋子）驱动Chern绝缘体与分数量子Chern绝缘体失稳，并给出统一的基于激发的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强电子-声子耦合会破坏Chern绝缘体（CI）和分数量子Chern绝缘体（FCI）而倾向于电荷有序，但CI与FCI中不稳定性的微观机制是否相同尚不清楚。本文旨在厘清两者是否共享统一的微观机制。

Method: 采用结合局域基优化（LBO）的密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）方法，研究平带Haldane模型与动力学Holstein声子在整数填充和分数填充下的耦合，分析声子对低能集体模式的缀饰效应。

Result: 发现声子的低能效应主要由其对电中性集体模式的缀饰主导。在CI和FCI中，声子分别将激子和磁旋子缀饰为复合模式，即激子极化子与磁旋子极化子。这些模式在接近相变时显著软化，其能量极小值对应的动量预示着电荷有序相的序波矢。声子谱从激子和磁旋子模式获得色散特征，成为这些中性激发的直接晶格信号。在FCI相中，电子-声子耦合还增强了引力子响应的相反手性谱权重，导致手性亏损。

Conclusion: 研究提供了CI与FCI中声子驱动不稳定性的统一、基于激发的图像，并确立晶格动力学可作为探测其中性集体模式的手段。

Abstract: Strong electron-phonon coupling can destabilize both Chern insulators (CIs) and fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) in favor of charge order, but whether the instabilities in CI and FCI share a common microscopic mechanism remains unclear. In this paper, we address this question by studying a flat-band Haldane model coupled to dynamical Holstein phonons at integer and fractional fillings using density-matrix renormalization group with local basis optimization. We find that the low-energy effects of phonons are dominated by their dressing of charge-neutral collective modes. In the CI and FCI, phonons dress excitons and magnetorotons into composite modes that we identify as exciton polarons and magnetoroton polarons, respectively. These modes soften strongly upon approaching the transition, while the momenta of their energy minima anticipate the ordering wave vectors of the charge-ordered phases. The phonon spectrum, in turn, acquires dispersive features inherited from the exciton and magnetoroton modes, providing direct lattice signatures of these neutral excitations. Within the FCI phase, electron-phonon coupling also enhances the opposite-chirality spectral weight of the graviton response, thereby resulting in a chirality deficit. Our results provide a unified excitation-based picture of phonon-driven instabilities in CIs and FCIs and establish lattice dynamics as a probe of their neutral collective modes.

</details>


### [157] [Excitonic Stripe Order in the Two-Orbital Hubbard-Kanamori Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36051)
*Rafael D. Soares,Luke Staszewski,Chunhan Feng,Alexander Wietek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了双轨道Hubbard-Kanamori模型中激子条纹序的形成机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子凝聚与条纹序是电子关联的两种不同表现，但两者的结合——激子条纹序——尚未被系统研究。

Method: 采用无约束实空间Hartree-Fock计算，辅以随机相近似（RPA）不稳定性分析，考察Kanamori相互作用如何选择不同激子通道。

Result: Hund交换符号决定凝聚体的自旋性质：铁磁Hund耦合倾向三重态激子序，反铁磁Hund耦合稳定单态激子序；掺杂后出现非公度激子条纹及三重态螺旋激子密度波；晶体场劈裂通过改变轨道嵌套显著重组激子不稳定性，甚至可在无配对跃迁时稳定三重态激子序。

Conclusion: 激子条纹是多轨道关联体系中一种独特的对称破缺态，本文明确了其稳定化的微观路径。

Abstract: Excitonic condensation and stripe formation are two distinct manifestations of electronic correlations. While excitonic order naturally arises in multi-orbital systems, stripe order is a prominent feature of doped correlated-electron models. Here, we investigate an excitonic analogue of stripe order in the two--orbital Hubbard-Kanamori model on the square lattice, characterized by a spatial modulation of inter-orbital particle-hole coherence and, in the orbital-parity-symmetric limit, spontaneous breaking of a relative orbital $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry. Using unrestricted real-space Hartree-Fock calculations complemented by random-phase-approximation instability analysis, we determine how the Kanamori interactions select different excitonic channels. The sign of Hund's exchange controls the spin character of the condensate: ferromagnetic Hund coupling favors triplet excitonic order, whereas antiferromagnetic Hund coupling stabilizes singlet excitonic order. Upon doping, commensurate excitonic density waves develop incommensurate textures, including excitonic stripes and, in the triplet sector, spiral excitonic density waves. We further show that crystal-field splitting strongly reorganizes the excitonic instability by modifying inter-orbital nesting and can stabilize triplet excitonic order even in the absence of pair hopping. Our results establish excitonic stripes as a distinct symmetry-broken state of multi-orbital correlated systems and identify microscopic routes for their stabilization.

</details>


### [158] [Magnetic excitations of the strongly-anisotropic triangular XXZ model: a projected spin-product state approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36140)
*Achille Mauri,Frédéric Mila*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种"投影"相干态近似方法，用于研究强Ising型各向异性的自旋-1/2三角XXZ模型，并与钴酸盐K2Co(SeO3)2实验数据进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验激发了人们对强Ising型各向异性自旋-1/2三角XXZ模型的基态性质和激发谱的研究兴趣，需要一种能在此强约束Hilbert空间内描述激发的新理论近似方法。

Method: 将自旋乘积态投影到三角Ising反铁磁体的低能构型上，构造"投影"相干态近似；在该近似下讨论基态序结构和纵向磁场下的磁化曲线，并通过从含时变分原理导出的运动方程线性化来讨论激发谱。

Result: 在零场下，变分近似给出一个非常平坦的准简并态势能面，导致激发谱中出现低能赝Goldstone模式。与线性自旋波理论相比，该近似结果有显著改善，但在赝Goldstone能隙数值和低场下M点附近的色散上仍存在偏差。

Conclusion: 该"投影"相干态近似能自然描述强约束Hilbert空间内的自旋波式激发，相较线性自旋波理论显著改善，但与KCSO实验相比在赝Goldstone能隙和低场M点色散上仍有残余偏差。

Abstract: Recent experiments have sparked interest on the ground-state properties and on the excitation spectrum of the spin-$1/2$ triangular XXZ model with strong Ising-like anisotropy. In this work, we introduce and analyze a ``projected'' coherent-state approximation to the model, constructed by projecting spin-product states onto the low-energy configurations of the triangular Ising antiferromagnet. Within this approximation, we discuss the structure of the ground-state ordering and the magnetization curve under a longitudinal field. We then discuss the excitation spectrum by linearizing equations of motion deriving from a time-dependent variational principle. This leads to a natural description of spin-wave-like excitations within the strongly constrained Hilbert space. At zero field, the variational approximation leads to a very flat landscape of quasi-degenerate states, which results in a low-energy pseudo-Goldstone mode in the excitation spectrum. We compare our results to experimental measurements on the cobaltite K$_{2}$Co(SeO$_{3}$)$_{2}$ (KCSO). Our approximation leads to a dramatic improvement as compared to linear spin-wave theory. However, there remain deviations in the value of the pseudo-Goldstone gap and in the dispersion near $M$ points at low field.

</details>


### [159] [Ergodicity in a hole-dopped Anderson spin insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36936)
*M. S. Bahovadinov,A. A. Markov,G. V. Shlyapnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在随机磁场下的一维$t-J_{XX}$模型中，单个空穴在弱无序极限下可使局域的自旋链热化，兼具遍历性与空穴离域；强磁场下空穴最终以有限局域长度局域，恢复Anderson自旋绝缘体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用多体系统在无序下发生局域化时，无法充当自身的热浴。电子系统中电荷与自旋自由度可互为热浴，因此探究Anderson绝缘体中自旋扇区在电荷掺杂（引入载流子）下的稳定性具有重要意义。

Method: 研究一维格点上的双组分费米子，采用强在位排斥极限（$t-J_{XX}$模型），并在随机磁场中引入单个空穴。对无空穴时任意弱随机磁场已局域的自旋扇区，考察引入空穴后的热化行为，并结合数值计算加以验证。

Result: 无空穴时，自旋扇区在任意弱随机磁场下均局域化。引入单个空穴后，在弱无序极限下空穴使自旋链热化；数值结果指示整个系统呈遍历性且空穴离域。随机磁场增大到一定程度后，空穴以有限局域长度局域化，Anderson自旋绝缘体得以恢复。

Conclusion: 单个空穴即可在弱无序下破坏自旋扇区的Anderson局域化并驱动热化，但该效应存在无序阈值：强无序时空穴重新局域，自旋绝缘体恢复，体现了电荷掺杂对自旋局域化稳定性的非单调调控。

Abstract: An interacting many-body system being localized in the presence of disorder can fail to serve as its own bath. In an electronic system, the charge and spin degrees of freedom can act as thermal baths for each other. In this paper, we study the stability of an Anderson insulator in the spin sector with respect to charge doping. We consider one-dimensional two-component fermions in a lattice with large onsite repulsion ($t-J_{XX}$ model) and with a single hole in a random magnetic field. In the absence of the hole the spin sector is localized in arbitrarily weak random magnetic fields. We argue that a single hole thermalizes the spin chain in the weak disorder limit. In this regime our numerical results indicate ergodicity of the system as a whole and delocalization of the hole. At larger magnetic fields the hole eventually localizes with a finite localization length, and the Anderson spin insulator is restored.

</details>


### [160] [Magnetic properties and Fermi-surface topology of kagome magnet HoV$_6$Sn$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36258)
*Tucker Beekmann,Ganesh Pokharel,Wyatt Edward Ackerman,Stephen D. Wilson,David E. Graf,Keshav Shrestha*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文结合磁化测量、隧道二极管振荡（TDO）实验与第一性原理计算，研究了笼目磁体HoV₆Sn₆的磁性和费米面拓扑。实验发现Tc≈2.4 K的长程磁有序及显著磁各向异性；TDO测得多个量子振荡频率（可达~9 kT），确定了费米面几何与回旋有效质量；计算表明V-d轨道主导费米能级附近的态密度，支持Ho磁矩沿c轴的铁磁基态，且计算的振荡频率与实验总体吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 笼目磁体因兼具磁性与拓扑电子结构而受到关注，HoV₆Sn₆的磁各向异性和费米面拓扑尚不清楚，需通过实验与理论结合揭示其低能电子性质。

Method: 采用温度与场依赖的磁化测量确定磁有序和磁各向异性；在最高41.5 T磁场、最低0.35 K温度下进行TDO测量以探测量子振荡、费米面几何和回旋有效质量；并开展第一性原理计算电子能带、态密度和费米面。

Result: 高温磁化遵循Curie-Weiss行为，Tc≈2.4 K以下出现长程磁有序；饱和磁矩约9.5（垂直面外）和7 μB/Ho（面内）；TDO显示多个量子振荡频率，最高约9 kT；能带呈现平带、van Hove奇点和近费米能级的Dirac型交叉；费米能级附近态密度由V-d轨道主导；计算支持Ho磁矩沿c轴的铁磁基态，与中子散射一致，计算振荡频率与实验总体吻合。

Conclusion: 实验与理论结合揭示了HoV₆Sn₆的磁各向异性和多带费米面拓扑，表明V笼目层主导其低能电子性质。

Abstract: We report a study of the magnetic properties and Fermi-surface topology of the kagome magnet HoV$_6$Sn$_6$ using magnetization and tunnel-diode-oscillator (TDO) measurements. The temperature-dependent magnetization exhibits Curie-Weiss behavior at high temperatures and reveals long-range magnetic order below $T_c$ $\approx$ 2.4 K. Below Tc, the field-dependent magnetization reveals pronounced magnetic anisotropy, with saturation moments of $\approx$ 9.5 and 7 $μ_B$ /Ho for fields applied along the out-of-plane and in-plane directions, respectively. To probe the Fermi surface, we perform TDO measurements in magnetic fields up to 41.5 T and at temperatures down to 0.35 K. The TDO signal exhibits well-defined quantum oscillations with multiple frequencies extending up to $\sim$ 9 kT. Angular- and temperature-dependent measurements are used to determine the Fermi-surface geometry and cyclotron effective masses, respectively. To elucidate the electronic structure, we perform first-principles calculations of the electronic bands, density of states (DOS), and Fermi surface. The calculated band structure exhibits flat-band features, van Hove singularities, and Dirac-like crossings near the Fermi level. The DOS near the Fermi level is dominated by the V-d orbitals, indicating that the V kagome layers play a major role in determining the low-energy electronic properties of HoV6Sn6. Our calculations further support a ferromagnetic ground state with the Ho moments oriented along the c axis, consistent with neutron-scattering measurements. The calculated quantum-oscillation frequencies from the Fermi-surface pockets of HoV6Sn6 are in overall good agreement with the experimentally observed frequencies. Our combined experimental and theoretical results provide insight into the magnetic anisotropy and electronic structure of HoV$_6$Sn$_6$ and reveal its multiband Fermi-surface topology.

</details>


### [161] [Quantum metric induced nonlinear transport in the hidden loop-current phase of kagome metal RbV$_3$Sb$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36353)
*Jia-Chen Shi,Shupeng Xu,Utkarsh Khandelwal,Nashra Pistawala,Luminita Harnagea,Steven J. May,Ritesh Agarwal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用三阶非线性输运探测RbV₃Sb₅中的隐藏低温相，在约35 K观察到纵向立方响应的拐点与方向各向异性及横向立方响应的奇磁场分量同时出现，揭示时间反演对称性破缺的电子重构，并指出量子度规四极子对纵向三阶响应的直接贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome金属AV₃Sb₅中可能存在的环流序隐藏低温相，其现有实验证据微弱且间接，亟需更灵敏、直接的探测手段。

Method: 采用三阶非线性输运测量，在RbV₃Sb₅中探测纵向与横向立方响应，分析其温度依赖性、方向各向异性及对常规弛豫时间标度的偏离，并结合环流电荷密度波模型进行理论解释。

Result: 在约35 K时，纵向立方响应出现强拐点并伴随显著方向各向异性，明显偏离常规弛豫时间标度；同时横向立方响应出现奇磁场分量。两者起始温度一致。理论表明量子度规四极子直接贡献纵向三阶响应，在环流电荷密度波模型中其方向选择性增强可再现观测到的角度重构。

Conclusion: 更高阶非线性输运可将电子对称性与量子度规的细微变化转化为可测量电学信号，为研究量子材料中复杂电子相提供有效手段。

Abstract: A hidden low-temperature phase with possible loop-current order has been proposed in the kagome metals AV$_3$Sb$_5$ (A=K, Rb or Cs), but its experimental signatures remain subtle and indirect. Here, we use third-order nonlinear transport to probe this hidden phase in RbV$_3$Sb$_5$. At ~35 K, the longitudinal cubic response develops a strong kink and pronounced directional anisotropy with a strong departure from common relaxation time scaling, while the transverse cubic response acquires a magnetic field-odd component at the same temperature. Their coincident onset identifies the third-harmonic response as a sensitive marker of the low-temperature electronic reconstruction with time-reversal broken symmetry. Crucially, we find that the quantum metric quadrupole contributes directly to the longitudinal third-order response. In a loop-current charge-density-wave model, its direction selective enhancement captures the observed angular reconstruction. Our work shows how higher-order nonlinear transport translates subtle changes in electronic symmetry and quantum metric into measurable electrical signatures in quantum materials that is important for studying complex electronic phases of matter.

</details>


### [162] [Artificial versus Natural Atoms: The uncanny capability of the many-body Schrödinger equation to produce emergent behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36356)
*Constantine Yannouleas*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 综述25年来“人造原子/分子”中维格纳分子物理的理论与实验进展：有限个相互排斥的费米子或玻色子被约束时，强关联导致粒子局域化，形成体态维格纳晶体的有限量子类比，并产生能隙淬灭、转振谱、纠缠及强磁场下的分数量子霍尔替代理论等效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天然原子/分子的量子多体问题难以精确求解，而近期可制备的量子纳米器件与囚禁体系提供了可控的有限粒子强关联平台。系统梳理维格纳分子作为“人造原子与分子”新物理的统一图像，有助于理解强关联本质、推动量子信息与量子计算应用、并深化基础多体物理。

Method: 以多体薛定谔方程（MBSE）的求解为核心理论工具，考察势能主导动能（强关联区）、高磁场或快速旋转等条件下费米子/玻色子有限体系的解；结合作者综合的二维半导体与莫尔过渡金属硫族化物量子点、单势阱/多势阱可形状调节的囚禁超冷中性原子或离子等实验装置进展；并与体态维格纳晶体及分数量子霍尔效应进行类比与对照。

Result: 综述表明：维格纳分子化产生若干显著量子效应，包括谱能隙的强烈淬灭、类天然分子的转动—振动谱、纠缠以及外扰导致的钉扎；在高磁场或快速旋转下，维格纳分子为分数量子霍尔效应提供替代理论；相关物理可由强关联区的MBSE解统一导出。

Conclusion: 过去25年的理论与实验进展确立了维格纳分子作为“人造原子与分子”新物理的统一框架。该领域兼具基础多体物理意义与量子信息/量子计算应用前景；其核心机制是强相互作用（势能或磁场/旋转主导）驱动的局域化与关联效应，值得进一步发展。

Abstract: The paper reviews the theoretical and experimental progress achieved in the last 25 years in understanding the novel physics of artificial atoms and molecules as arising from the formation of Wigner molecules (WMs) of localized (to a stronger or lesser extent) fermionic or bosonic particles, which are finite quantum analogs of the more familiar bulk Wigner crystal. The term artificial atoms, as used here, encompasses a broad range of recently fabricated quantum nanodevices and experimental apparatuses consisting of a finite number of mutually repelling confined particles, including two-dimensional semiconductor and moiré transition metal dichalcogenide quantum dots, as well as trapped ultracold neutral atoms or ions. These nano-sized or micro-sized artificial devices and apparatuses (in single well or multi-well of variable-shape arrangements) hold a great promise for technological applications in the field of quantum information and quantum computers, as well as for advances in fundamental many-body physics. Prominent quantum effects of Wigner molecularization are the strong quenching of the spectral energy gaps, the appearance of rovibational spectra (in analogy with natural molecules), entanglement, and pinning due to an external perturbation. In high magnetic fields or at rapid rotation, WMs provide an alternative theory to the fractional quantum Hall effect. The physics of Wigner molecules is shown to derive from the solutions of the many-body Schrödinger equation (MBSE) in the regime of strong interparticle correlations arising from the dominance of the potential over the kinetic energy, or from a high magnetic field, as well as from a rapid rotation... (continues in the paper).

</details>


### [163] [Neural-quantum-state based downfolding of the three-band Emery model for cuprates and nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38150)
*Hannah Lange,Julius F. A. Tirpitz,Annabelle Bohrdt*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出将隐藏费米子行列式态(HFDS)作为可扩展的神经量子态方法，用于求解铜氧化物与无限层镍酸盐的三带Emery模型，在10×10元胞(300格点)的二维系统上研究了基态与掺杂分布，并通过构造相互作用Wannier函数将三带模型下折叠为有效单带模型，发现有效模型显著偏离常规Fermi-Hubbard模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解铜氧化物及新近的无限层镍酸盐中高温超导的物理机制是凝聚态物理的核心难题。三带Emery模型被认为能描述这些材料铜氧/镍氧层的低能物理，但二维大尺度系统的精确求解在计算上极具挑战，亟需可扩展的变分方法；同时，多带模型向单带模型下折叠时是否可用常规Fermi-Hubbard模型描述尚不清楚。

Method: 采用神经量子态(NQS)中的隐藏费米子行列式态(HFDS)作为可扩展变分方法，求解二维三带Emery模型。首先在宽度为2的几何构型上与矩阵乘积态(MPS)进行基准对比；随后研究最大达10×10元胞(300格点)的完全二维系统基态；再通过构造相互作用Wannier函数将三带模型下折叠为有效单带描述，参数覆盖从铜氧化物相关的电荷转移区到镍酸盐的Hubbard-Mott区，并系统扫描电荷转移能隙。

Result: HFDS在宽度为2的构型上与MPS基准一致；在二维大系统中刻画了掺杂剂的动量空间分布，发现与铜氧化物实验相似的显著电子-空穴二象性。有效单带模型在所有参数区间均显著偏离常规Fermi-Hubbard模型：典型U/t比值被增强，部分参数出现明显的掺杂依赖性，并存在超出常规Hubbard模型的显著项，最突出的是密度辅助跃迁t_n。特别地，在所有有效模型中，t_n对粒子-空穴不对称性的贡献最大，而非次近邻跃迁项；有效参数敏感依赖于电荷转移能、掺杂和相互作用比。

Conclusion: HFDS被确立为研究二维Emery模型的高效工具，可实现对大尺度二维多带系统的可靠变分求解；同时表明单带描述可能需要引入源自底层多带模型的相互作用项，常规Fermi-Hubbard模型不足以刻画这些材料的有效低能物理。

Abstract: Understanding the physics underlying high-temperature superconductivity in cuprates and, more recently, infinite-layer nickelates has remained a central challenge in condensed-matter physics. We establish neural quantum states (NQS), specifically Hidden Fermion Determinant States (HFDS), as a scalable variational approach to the three-band Emery model of the copper- and nickel-oxide layers in these materials. After benchmarking HFDS against matrix product states on width-two geometries, we study ground states of fully two-dimensional (2D) systems of up to $10\times10$ unit cells ($300$ sites). We characterize the momentum-space distribution of dopants and find a pronounced electron-hole dichotomy similar to cuprate experiments. We further downfold the three-band model to effective single-band descriptions by constructing interacting Wannier functions. We consider a wide range of parameters -- from the charge-transfer regime relevant to cuprates to the Hubbard-Mott regime of nickelates, as well as systematic scans of the charge-transfer gap that has been demonstrated to impact the critical superconducting temperatures. Across all regimes, the effective model significantly deviates from the usual Fermi-Hubbard model: The typical ratio $U/t$ is enhanced, some parameters experience a significant doping dependence, and sizable terms beyond the conventional Hubbard model are present, most notably a density-assisted hopping $t_n$. Notably, in all effective models, $t_n$ has the largest contribution to particle-hole asymmetry, rather than next-nearest-neighbor hopping contributions. The effective parameters sensitively depend on the charge-transfer energy, doping, and interaction ratios. Our results establish HFDS as an efficient tool for studying the 2D Emery model and demonstrate that single-band descriptions can require interaction terms generated by the underlying multi-band models.

</details>


### [164] [Probing the electronic structure of $\mathrm{UTe}_2$ with ARPES and high-energy spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36706)
*Shin-ichi Fujimori*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 综述了UTe₂的多种光谱学研究，指出其电子结构存在显著的技术依赖性，并支持5f²/5f³混合价基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTe₂因其非常规超导及可能的自旋三重态、拓扑非平庸配对态而成为研究热点，但U 5f电子性质及其在低能准粒子态中的参与仍是核心未解问题。

Method: 综述动量分辨角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）以及元素与构型敏感的X射线探针，包括XAS、XANES、XMCD、RXES和RIXS，并结合DFT+DMFT等现代电子结构计算进行综合分析。

Result: 推断出的电子结构表现出显著的技术依赖性；整合各类光谱结果与理论计算后，支持UTe₂存在5f²与5f³构型显著混合的中间价基态。

Conclusion: 该综述勾画出将正常态电子结构与超导机制相连接所需的关键实验与理论基准。

Abstract: $\mathrm{UTe}_2$ has emerged as one of the most intensively studied strongly correlated materials in recent years owing to its unconventional superconductivity and the possible realization of a spin-triplet, topologically nontrivial pairing state. A central open issue concerns the nature of the $\mathrm{U}\,5f$ electrons and their participation in low-energy quasiparticle states. In this review, we summarize recent spectroscopic studies of $\mathrm{UTe}_2$ using momentum-resolved angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and element- and configuration-sensitive X-ray probes, including X-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS), X-ray absorption near-edge structure (XANES), X-ray magnetic circular dichroism (XMCD), resonant X-ray emission spectroscopy (RXES), and resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS). A key finding is the pronounced technique dependence of the inferred electronic structure. We synthesize the present spectroscopic picture by integrating these results with modern electronic-structure calculations such as density functional theory plus dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT). These findings support an intermediate-valence ground state with significant admixture of $5f^2$ and $5f^3$ configurations in $\mathrm{UTe}_2$, and they delineate key experimental and theoretical benchmarks needed to connect the normal-state electronic structure to the superconducting mechanism.

</details>


### [165] [Quantized Hyperfine Field at an Implanted $μ^+$ Site in PrPb$_3$: Interplay between Localized $f$ Electrons and an Interstitial Charged Particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36868)
*T. U. Ito,W. Higemoto,K. Ohishi,N. Nishida,R. H. Heffner,Y. Aoki,A. Amato,T. Onimaru,H. S. Suzuki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在PrPb₃中利用μ⁺自旋旋转与弛豫研究了间隙氢样粒子对局域f电子的影响，首次在f电子化合物中观测到自发μ⁺自旋进动，并归因于μ⁺与最近邻¹⁴¹Pr超精细增强核自旋的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间隙氢样粒子（如μ⁺）如何改变局域f电子态尚未明确，该研究旨在通过μSR探测PrPb₃中间隙带电粒子对f电子局域性质的影响。

Method: 采用μ⁺自旋旋转与弛豫（μSR）技术，测量PrPb₃中自发μ⁺自旋进动信号，并分析其与最近邻¹⁴¹Pr核自旋（具Ising型各向异性、经超精细增强）的耦合及自旋动力学。

Result: 首次在f电子化合物中观察到具有谐波频率的自发μ⁺自旋进动；信号源于μ⁺与最近邻¹⁴¹Pr超精细增强核自旋的耦合；与体态¹⁴¹Pr相比，最近邻¹⁴¹Pr的自旋动力学被显著抑制。

Conclusion: 上述结果强烈表明，间隙带电粒子导致PrPb₃中f电子态发生改变，为间隙粒子调控f电子局域态提供了微观证据。

Abstract: The local effect of an interstitial hydrogen-like particle on localized $f$ electrons was studied in PrPb$_3$ by means of $μ^+$ spin rotation and relaxation. Spontaneous $μ^+$ spin precession with harmonic frequencies was observed for the first time in $f$ electron compounds. We demonstrate that the signal is derived from a coupling between the $μ^+$ spin and the hyperfine-enhanced nuclear spin of nearest neighbor (nn) $^{141}$Pr with Ising-like anisotropy. The signal also suggests a marked suppression of spin dynamics of the nn $^{141}$Pr in comparison with that of the bulk $^{141}$Pr. These facts strongly indicate modification of the $f$ electronic state due to the interstitial charged particle.

</details>


### [166] [Evolution of Magnetism in Ce$_4$Ge$_7$ under Magnetic Field and Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37129)
*Kaixin Ye,Qihe Yu,Yongjian Li,Yanan Zhang,Ye Chen,Rui Li,Lin Jiao,M. Smidman,Yongjun Zhang,Yu Liu,Huiqiu Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文报道了非中心对称正交结构单晶Ce4Ge7的磁性与输运性质，发现其在7.3 K发生反铁磁转变，b轴磁场下1.3 T出现由本征Karplus-Luttinger机制主导的异常霍尔效应，并在10.2 GPa附近可能存在反铁磁量子临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非中心对称晶体中磁性与输运性质的关联，特别是反铁磁量子临界点的存在及其对输运行为的影响，为理解量子临界现象提供新体系。

Method: 对单晶Ce4Ge7进行磁化与输运测量，施加不同方向磁场观察磁性和霍尔效应；并施加静水压至10.2 GPa以上，研究反铁磁转变的演化及相关输运系数变化。

Result: Ce4Ge7在TN=7.3 K发生反铁磁转变；b轴磁场下2 K时1.3 T出现超磁转变，并伴随由Karplus-Luttinger本征机制主导的异常霍尔效应；静水压下TN先略增后被逐渐抑制，Pc=10.2 GPa以上磁性序消失；在Pc附近观察到A系数发散、剩余电阻率极大值及非费米液体行为。

Conclusion: Ce4Ge7中可能存在的反铁磁量子临界点，可通过输运异常与磁有序抑制的协同变化加以表征，表明该体系是研究量子临界行为的候选材料。

Abstract: We report the magnetic and transport properties of single-crystalline Ce$_4$Ge$_7$, which crystallizes in a non-centrosymmetric orthorhombic structure (space group C222$_1$). It undergoes an antiferromagnetic transition at $T_N$ = 7.3 K. When a magnetic field is applied along the $b$-axis, a metamagnetic transition occurs at 1.3 T (at 2 K). Correspondingly, this transition gives rise to an anomalous Hall effect, which is dominated by the intrinsic Karplus-Luttinger mechanism. Under hydrostatic pressure, $T_N$ of Ce$_4$Ge$_7$ initially increases slightly and is then gradually suppressed. For pressures above $P_c$ = 10.2 GPa, no magnetic order is observed. The divergence of the $A$ coefficient, the maximum of the residual resistivity, and the non-Fermi liquid behavior around $P_c$ indicate the possible existence of an antiferromagnetic quantum critical point in Ce$_4$Ge$_7$.

</details>


### [167] [Uniform-Charge-Density $d$-Wave Superconductivity in the Pure $t$-$J$ Model at $1/8$ Doping on an Infinite Cylinder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37191)
*Guangyu Yu,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用VUMPS方法在热力学极限下研究正方晶格t-J模型1/8空穴掺杂基态，发现均匀电荷密度的d波超导基态，无条纹或磁序，为长期争议提供方法学上独立的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正方晶格t-J模型在1/8空穴掺杂下的基态长期存在争议：数值研究结果相互矛盾，一部分报告条纹序，另一部分报告超导电性。有限圆柱计算中还存在边界钉扎效应，可能干扰结论，因此需要一种在热力学极限下直接优化、消除边界效应的方法来澄清该争论。

Method: 采用变分均匀矩阵乘积态（VUMPS）方法，沿圆柱轴方向在热力学极限下直接优化波函数，消除有限圆柱计算的边界钉扎效应。在周长L_y=8的无限圆柱上进行计算，分析配对关联、电荷与自旋关联；并通过扭曲边界条件（twisted-boundary-condition）计算相刚度，观察序参量相位随外加磁通的绕转行为。

Result: 在L_y=8无限圆柱上发现均匀电荷密度的d波超导基态，具有准长程配对关联；主导配对出现在零质心动量处，未检测到显著的有限动量配对分量。扭曲边界条件计算显示超导相刚度有限，序参量相位随外加磁通平滑绕转。电荷与自旋关联快速衰减，无长程条纹或磁序迹象。相比之下，同一方法在L_y=6、1/6掺杂的圆柱上可明确识别出条纹序。

Conclusion: 结果为纯t-J模型中长期的条纹—超导争论提供了方法学上独立的证据，揭示了掺杂Mott绝缘体最小模型中的均匀d波超导电性，支持1/8掺杂下超导而非条纹的基态图像。

Abstract: The ground state of the square-lattice $t$-$J$ model at $1/8$ hole doping has been the subject of a long-standing controversy, with conflicting numerical reports of stripe order versus superconductivity. Here, we address this debate using the variational uniform matrix product state (VUMPS) method, which optimizes the wave function directly in the thermodynamic limit along the cylinder axis and eliminates the boundary pinning effects that can arise in finite-cylinder calculations. On an infinite cylinder of circumference $L_y = 8$, we find a uniform-charge-density $d$-wave superconducting ground state with quasi-long-range pairing correlations. The dominant pairing occurs at zero center-of-mass momentum, while no sizable finite-momentum pairing component is detected. Twisted-boundary-condition calculations reveal a finite superconducting phase stiffness, with the order-parameter phase winding smoothly to follow the applied flux, providing an independent probe of the robustness of the superconductivity. Moreover, both charge and spin correlations decay rapidly, with no signatures of long-range stripe or magnetic order, in contrast to an $L_y=6$ cylinder at $1/6$ doping where the same method readily identifies stripe order. Our results provide methodologically distinct evidence in the long-standing stripe--superconductivity debate in the pure $t$-$J$ model and reveal uniform $d$-wave superconductivity in a minimal model of doped Mott insulators.

</details>


### [168] [Correlation-assisted topological and metamagnetic transitions in Rashba-coupled superconductors: $t$-$J$-$U$ model study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37812)
*Tushar Dey,Maciej Fidrysiak,Józef Spałek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用SGA变分方法研究含Rashba自旋轨道耦合(RSOC)的t-J-U模型，构建相图并刻画拓扑超导态，发现弱关联主导的C=±4态与关联驱动的C=±2拓扑超导态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物等强电子关联体系中已观察到与Rashba型自旋轨道耦合(RSOC)相容的自旋-动量锁定，因此需要研究电子关联与RSOC对铜氧化物及相关超导体配对的联合效应。

Method: 采用统计自洽变分近似(SGA)，在方晶格t-J-U模型中加入RSOC，构建相图；并通过Zeeman场引入时间反演对称破缺，刻画涌现的拓扑超导(TSC)态，同时与弱耦合Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG)结果对比。

Result: 在接近半满的宽泛在位库仑排斥区间发现Chern数C=±4的TSC态，该态不由关联主导，弱耦合BdG方案亦可得到；此外识别出一个由电子关联驱动、经由无体能隙闭合的拓扑相变产生的C=±2 TSC态，并伴随不连续的变磁转变与Lifshitz转变；在金属-绝缘体转变之上，混合宇称超导序参量的d波与p波分量呈现定性不同的掺杂演化。

Conclusion: RSOC耦合超导体的相图必须考虑超出BdG方案的关联与RSOC联合效应，关联可驱动具有独特拓扑性质的超导态。

Abstract: Unconventional superconductivity commonly emerges in systems characterized by strong electronic correlations, with layered copper-oxides serving as a canonical example. Observation of spin-momentum locking within Bi-family of the cuprates, compatible with the presence of non-negligible Rashba-type spin-orbit coupling (RSOC), calls for an investigation of the joint effects of electronic correlations and RSOC on pairing in copper-oxide and related superconductors. Employing statistically-consistent variational approximation (SGA), we carry out such an analysis by constructing the phase diagram for the case of square-lattice \textit{t-J-U} model incorporating RSOC. We also investigate the effects of time-reversal-symmetry breaking by Zeeman field, as well as characterize emergent topological superconducting (TSC) states. Chern number $C = \pm 4$ TSC is found in a broad regime of on-site Coulomb repulsion close to half-filling. The latter is not governed by correlations and emerges also within the weak-coupling Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG) scheme. Yet, we identify a distinct $C = \pm 2$ TSC state that is driven specifically by electronic correlations via a topological transition occurring with no bulk quasiparticle gap closure, and is accompanied by discontinuous metamagnetic and Lifshitz transitions. Moreover, a qualitatively distinct doping evolution of the $d$- and $p$-wave components of the underlying mixed-parity SC order parameter above the metal-to-insulator transition is demonstrated. Our work points toward the relevance of joint correlation and RSOC effects beyond BdG scheme to phase diagrams of RSOC-coupled superconductors.

</details>


### [169] [Strong-to-Weak Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking of Dephased Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37965)
*Abhijat Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限密度退相干下具有U(1)对称性的费米子系统，建立了强-弱自发对称破缺(SWSSB)与紧致XY模型之间的映射关系，并系统分析了不同维度、不同母态下SWSSB的相图与普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强-弱自发对称破缺(SWSSB)是混合态中伴随全局电荷信息丢失而出现的新型相变。作者希望理解费米子体系在无限密度退相干下所产生的混合态的SWSSB性质，并建立分析此类相变的通用理论框架。

Method: 核心观察：在无限退相干下，每个Renyi关联函数精确等于紧致XY模型的spin-spin关联函数，其Boltzmann权重为经典系综满计数统计生成函数的平方（Renyi-2对应母态本身的满计数统计）。对Renyi-1关联函数发展了适用于任意对角混合态的新型图解展开，计算Renyi-1 XY模型的耦合。对偶数Renyi指数量子发展了复制场论，可适用于有限退相干情形。

Result: 完全退相干的金属在d≥2显示长程SWSSB（d=2获数值与实验确认），在d=1显示准长程SWSSB。绝缘母态在无限退相干下依UV细节可存在SWSSB相或平庸相。d≥3的长程SWSSB与d=2的准长程SWSSB在半普适、依赖Renyi指数的阈值之上存活，当母态深入绝缘相时消失，例外是量子霍尔绝缘体普遍具有准长程SWSSB。对偶数Renyi指数还确定了远离无限退相干时SWSSB相的稳定性，并导出了SWSSB相变的普适类。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将无限退相干下的Renyi关联函数映射到紧致XY模型，为混合态SWSSB提供了统一的场论框架，揭示了金属与绝缘母态在SWSSB行为上的本质差异，并给出了相图、阈值及相变普适类。

Abstract: Strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB) is a novel phase transition in mixed states associated with the loss of global charge information. In this work we study fermions with $U(1)$ symmetry under infinite density dephasing and derive the SWSSB properties of the resulting mixed states. Our central observation is that at infinite dephasing every Renyi correlator is exactly the spin-spin correlator of a compact XY model, whose Boltzmann weight is the squared full-counting-statistics generating function of a classical ensemble built from the parent state. For the Renyi-2 correlator it is the full counting statistics of the parent state itself. We complement the infinite dephasing mapping with a novel diagrammatic expansion for the Renyi-1 correlator, applicable to any diagonal mixed state, which computes the couplings of the Renyi-1 XY model. We also study a replica field theory for even Renyi index quantities, applicable at finite dephasing. Our analysis indicates that fully dephased metals display long-range SWSSB in $d\geq 2$, as confirmed numerically and experimentally in $d=2$, and quasi-long-range SWSSB in $d=1$. For insulating parent states both an SWSSB and a trivial phase are possible at infinite dephasing depending on UV details. Long-range SWSSB in $d\geq 3$, or quasi-long-range SWSSB in $d=2$, survives above a nonuniversal, Renyi-index-dependent threshold and is lost when the parent state is deep in the insulating phase, with the exception of quantum Hall insulators which generically have quasi-long-range SWSSB. For even Renyi indices we also determine the stability of the SWSSB phase away from infinite dephasing and derive the universality class of the SWSSB transition.

</details>


### [170] [Variational Monte Carlo for the Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38015)
*Luca F. Tocchio*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本讲义介绍了Hubbard模型的变分蒙特卡洛方法，涵盖单轨道与多轨道模型、变分波函数、蒙特卡洛计算、随机重构优化及相关关联函数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Hubbard模型中超导态与磁态等强关联电子态提供一个系统的变分蒙特卡洛方法介绍，作为教学性质的讲义材料。

Method: 先简要引入单轨道Hubbard模型及其多轨道扩展（仅限电子模型），随后阐述变分蒙特卡洛的一般框架，给出能恰当描述单/多轨道Hubbard模型中超导态与磁态的变分波函数，并详述针对给定波函数的蒙特卡洛计算流程，以及用随机重构法（stochastic reconfiguration）优化变分波函数的方法。

Result: 给出了可在变分蒙特卡洛中计算的若干关联函数结果，可用于判定电荷序、自旋序及超导性的存在。

Conclusion: 该讲义系统梳理了Hubbard模型变分蒙特卡洛方法的理论框架、波函数构造、计算与优化流程，并通过关联函数结果展示了其在识别电荷/自旋序和超导性方面的应用。

Abstract: In these lecture notes, we introduce the variational Monte Carlo method for the Hubbard model. First, we briefly introduce the single-orbital Hubbard model and its multi-orbital extension, focusing only on electronic models. Then, we introduce the general scheme under which variational Monte Carlo works and we present the variational wave functions that can properly describe superconductive and magnetic states in single-orbital and multi-orbital Hubbard models. We also detail how to perform Monte Carlo calculations for the given wave functions. We also describe how to optimize a variational wave function with the stochastic reconfiguration method. Finally, we present a few results for correlation functions that can be computed in variational Monte Carlo, to determine, for instance, the presence of charge or spin order and superconductivity.

</details>


### [171] [Boundary Criticality in (2+1)-dimensional U(1) Dirac Quantum Spin Liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38088)
*Huan Jiang,Zhiming Pan,Xue-Jia Yu,Shao-Kai Jian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在(2+1)维U(1)狄拉克自旋液体中，涌现规范场的Neumann与Dirichlet边界条件导致不同的边界普适类，可通过可调边界相互作用的格点模型实现与探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涌现规范场可在不改变体能量子自旋液体的前提下控制边界临界行为，但对其边界临界性的理解尚不充分，需要明确不同边界条件下是否产生不同的边界普适类及其可观测表征。

Method: 针对低能物理由无质量量子电动力学描述的(2+1)维U(1)狄拉克自旋液体，在半空间中进行微扰重整化群分析。比较涌现规范场的Neumann与Dirichlet边界条件，确定基本场与规范不变算符的边界标度维度，并提出具有可调边界相互作用的费米子-规范格点模型。

Result: Neumann与Dirichlet边界条件对应不同的边界普适类；得到基本场及规范不变算符的边界标度维度，可作为可观测信号；构造了可用于实现和探测这些边界普适类的微观费米子-规范格点模型。

Conclusion: 涌现规范场的边界条件可独立于体态调控边界临界行为，形成不同的边界普适类；所给标度维度与格点模型为数值和实验探测提供途径。

Abstract: An emergent gauge field can control boundary critical behavior without changing the bulk quantum spin liquid. We investigate the boundary criticality in $(2+1)$-dimensional $U(1)$ Dirac spin liquid, whose low-energy physics is described by massless quantum electrodynamics. Using a perturbative renormalization-group analysis in a half-space, we show that Neumann and Dirichlet boundary conditions for the emergent gauge field lead to distinct boundary universality classes. We determine the boundary scaling dimensions of the fundamental fields and gauge-invariant operators that provide observable signatures. We further propose a fermion-gauge lattice model with tunable boundary interactions as a microscopic setting for realizing and probing these boundary universality classes.

</details>
